#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};

    #[test]
    fn test_strip_ansi() {
        assert_eq!(
            strip_ansi("hello \x1b[31mred\x1b[0m world"),
            "hello red world"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            strip_ansi("\x1b]8;;https://example.com\x07Link\x1b]8;;\x07"),
            "Link"
        );
        assert_eq!(strip_ansi("test\x1b_mehh:c\x07cursor"), "testcursor");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_visible_width() {
        assert_eq!(visible_width("hello"), 5);
        assert_eq!(visible_width("tab\tstop"), 3 + 3 + 4);
        assert_eq!(visible_width("hello\x1b[32m world\x1b[0m"), 11);
        assert_eq!(visible_width("🦀"), 2);
        assert_eq!(visible_width("你好"), 4);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_truncate_to_width() {
        assert_eq!(
            truncate_to_width("hello world", 8, "...", false),
            "hello..."
        );
        assert_eq!(truncate_to_width("hi", 5, "...", true), "hi   ");
        let styled = "\x1b[31mhello world\x1b[0m";
        let trunc = truncate_to_width(styled, 8, "...", false);
        assert!(trunc.contains("hello"));
        assert!(trunc.ends_with("\x1b[0m"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_wrap_text_with_ansi() {
        let text = "hello brave new world";
        let wrapped = wrap_text_with_ansi(text, 12);
        assert_eq!(wrapped.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(wrapped[0], "hello brave");
        assert_eq!(wrapped[1], "new world");

        let long = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";
        let wrapped_long = wrap_text_with_ansi(long, 10);
        assert_eq!(wrapped_long.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(wrapped_long[0], "abcdefghij");
        assert_eq!(wrapped_long[1], "klmnopqrst");
        assert_eq!(wrapped_long[2], "uvwxyz");

        let styled = "\x1b[1mhello world this is bold\x1b[0m";
        let wrapped_styled = wrap_text_with_ansi(styled, 12);
        assert!(wrapped_styled.len() >= 2);
        assert!(wrapped_styled[1].starts_with("\x1b[1m"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_slice_by_column() {
        let line = "abcdefgh";
        assert_eq!(slice_by_column(line, 2, 5), "cde");

        let styled = "\x1b[32mabcdef\x1b[0m";
        let sliced = slice_by_column(styled, 1, 4);
        assert_eq!(strip_ansi(&sliced), "bcd");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_normalize_crossterm_key() {
        let ev = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Char('c'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
        assert_eq!(normalize_crossterm_key(ev), Some("ctrl+c".to_string()));

        let ev_enter = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Enter, KeyModifiers::NONE);
        assert_eq!(normalize_crossterm_key(ev_enter), Some("enter".to_string()));

        let ev_shift_enter = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Enter, KeyModifiers::SHIFT);
        assert_eq!(
            normalize_crossterm_key(ev_shift_enter),
            Some("shift+enter".to_string())
        );

        let ev_alt_left = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Left, KeyModifiers::ALT);
        assert_eq!(
            normalize_crossterm_key(ev_alt_left),
            Some("alt+left".to_string())
        );

        let ev_tab = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Tab, KeyModifiers::NONE);
        assert_eq!(normalize_crossterm_key(ev_tab), Some("tab".to_string()));

        let ev_backtab = KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::BackTab, KeyModifiers::NONE);
        assert_eq!(
            normalize_crossterm_key(ev_backtab),
            Some("backtab".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_matches_key() {
        assert!(matches_key("ctrl+c", "ctrl+c"));
        assert!(matches_key("enter", "return"));
        assert!(matches_key("escape", "esc"));
        assert!(matches_key("shift+ctrl+p", "ctrl+shift+p"));
        assert!(matches_key("shift+tab", "backtab"));
        assert!(!matches_key("ctrl+c", "ctrl+v"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_keybindings() {
        let kb = Keybindings::default_bindings();
        assert!(kb.matches("ctrl+c", TuiAction::Interrupt));
        assert!(kb.matches("escape", TuiAction::Interrupt));
        assert!(kb.matches("enter", TuiAction::Submit));
        assert!(kb.matches("shift+enter", TuiAction::Newline));
        assert!(kb.matches("ctrl+w", TuiAction::DeleteWordBackward));
        assert!(kb.matches("ctrl+y", TuiAction::Yank));
        assert!(kb.matches("ctrl+z", TuiAction::Undo));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_composite_tui_line() {
        let base = "Hello World! This is a base line.";
        let overlay = "[POPUP]";
        let composite = composite_tui_line(base, overlay, 6, 30);
        let clean = strip_ansi(&composite);
        assert!(clean.starts_with("Hello [POPUP]"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_mock_terminal_and_renderer() {
        let mut term = MockTerminal::new(40, 10);
        let mut renderer = TuiRenderer::new();

        let lines = vec![
            format!("Line 1{CURSOR_MARKER}"),
            "Line 2".to_string(),
        ];
        renderer.render_frame(&mut term, lines, true).unwrap();

        let out = term.full_output();
        assert!(out.contains("\x1b[?2026h"));
        assert!(out.contains("Line 1"));
        assert!(out.contains("Line 2"));
        assert!(out.contains("\x1b[?2026l"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_components_basic() {
        let text = Text::new("Hello Text").with_padding(1);
        let lines = text.render(20);
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(lines[0], " Hello Text ");

        let spacer = Spacer::new(3);
        assert_eq!(spacer.render(20).len(), 3);

        let bbox = BorderedBox::new(Box::new(Text::new("Inner")))
            .with_title("Title")
            .with_style(BorderStyle::Rounded);
        let box_lines = bbox.render(20);
        assert!(box_lines.len() >= 3);
        assert!(box_lines[0].contains("Title"));
        assert!(box_lines[1].contains("Inner"));

        let mut loader = Loader::new("Loading...");
        assert!(loader.render(20)[0].contains("Loading..."));
        loader.tick();
        assert!(loader.render(20)[0].contains("Loading..."));

        let items = vec![
            SelectItem::new("1", "Apple").with_description("Fruit"),
            SelectItem::new("2", "Banana").with_description("Fruit"),
        ];
        let mut list = SelectList::new(items, 5);
        let list_rendered = list.render(30);
        assert!(list_rendered[0].contains('>'));
        assert!(list_rendered[0].contains("Apple"));
        list.move_down();
        let list_rendered2 = list.render(30);
        assert!(list_rendered2[1].contains('>'));
        assert!(list_rendered2[1].contains("Banana"));

        // Fuzzy filter: "bnn" matches Banana
        list.set_filter("bnn");
        assert_eq!(list.filtered_indices(), vec![1]);
        assert_eq!(list.selected_item().map(|it| it.label.as_str()), Some("Banana"));

        // Out-of-bounds selected should not panic when rendering
        list.selected = 100;
        let safe_lines = list.render(30);
        assert!(!safe_lines.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_editor_operations_and_paste_collapse() {
        let mut editor = Editor::new();
        editor.insert_text("hello");
        assert_eq!(editor.text(), "hello");

        // Test multiline paste collapsing (> 10 lines)
        let large_paste = "line1\nline2\nline3\nline4\nline5\nline6\nline7\nline8\nline9\nline10\nline11\nline12";
        editor.clear();
        editor.insert_text(large_paste);

        // Render buffer contains collapsed token
        let rendered = editor.render(80);
        assert!(rendered[0].contains("[paste #1 +12 lines]"));

        // But .text() expands to the full original payload!
        assert_eq!(editor.text(), large_paste);

        // Test undo
        assert!(editor.undo());
        assert_eq!(editor.text(), "");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_editor_autocomplete() {
        let mut editor = Editor::new();
        editor.set_text("/mo");
        editor.set_autocomplete(vec![
            SelectItem::new("model", "model"),
            SelectItem::new("mode", "mode"),
        ]);
        let rendered = editor.render(40);
        assert!(rendered.iter().any(|l| l.contains("model")));

        let kb = Keybindings::default_bindings();
        let out = editor.handle_editor_key("enter", &kb);
        assert!(matches!(out, EditorOutput::Changed(_)));
        assert!(editor.autocomplete.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_view() {
        let md_src = "# Heading 1\n\nThis is a paragraph with **bold** and *italic* and `code`.\n\n- item 1\n- item 2\n\n```rust\nfn main() {}\n```";
        let view = MarkdownView::new(md_src);
        let lines = view.render(50);
        assert!(!lines.is_empty());
        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("Heading 1")));
        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("item 1")));
        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("main()")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_theme_dark_and_light() {
        let dark = Theme::dark();
        assert_eq!(dark.name, "dark");
        assert!(!dark.accent.is_empty());
        assert!(!dark.border.is_empty());
        assert!(!dark.success.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(dark.fg(&dark.accent, "text"), format!("{}text\x1b[0m", dark.accent));
        assert_eq!(dark.fg("", "plain"), "plain");

        let light = Theme::light();
        assert_eq!(light.name, "light");
        assert_ne!(dark, light);
        assert_eq!(Theme::from_name("light"), light);
        assert_eq!(Theme::from_name("DARK"), dark);
        assert_eq!(Theme::from_name("unknown"), dark);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_hex_to_ansi() {
        assert_eq!(
            hex_to_fg_ansi("#00ff88"),
            Some("\x1b[38;2;0;255;136m".to_string())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            hex_to_bg_ansi("#00ff88"),
            Some("\x1b[48;2;0;255;136m".to_string())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            hex_to_fg_ansi("00ff88"),
            Some("\x1b[38;2;0;255;136m".to_string())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            hex_to_fg_ansi("#f00"),
            Some("\x1b[38;2;255;0;0m".to_string())
        );
        assert_eq!(hex_to_fg_ansi("not-a-color"), None);
        assert_eq!(hex_to_fg_ansi(""), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_theme_json_load_and_override() {
        let temp_dir = std::env::temp_dir();
        let test_theme_path = temp_dir.join("mehh_test_theme_override.json");

        let json_content = r##"{
            "name": "neon",
            "accent": "#00ff88",
            "border": "\u001b[35m",
            "selectedBg": "#112233",
            "mdHeading": "#ff5500"
        }"##;
        std::fs::write(&test_theme_path, json_content).unwrap();

        let loaded = Theme::load(Some(test_theme_path.to_str().unwrap()));
        assert_eq!(loaded.name, "neon");
        assert_eq!(loaded.accent, "\x1b[38;2;0;255;136m");
        assert_eq!(loaded.border, "\x1b[35m");
        assert_eq!(loaded.selected_bg, "\x1b[48;2;17;34;51m");
        assert_eq!(loaded.md_heading, "\x1b[38;2;255;85;0m");
        // Non-specified fields fall back to dark defaults
        assert_eq!(loaded.success, Theme::dark().success);

        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&test_theme_path);

        // Fallbacks for missing files or None
        assert_eq!(Theme::load(None), Theme::dark());
        assert_eq!(Theme::load(Some("non_existent_theme_98765")), Theme::dark());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_view_with_theme() {
        let mut custom_theme = Theme::dark();
        custom_theme.md_heading = "\x1b[38;2;255;0;128m".to_string();
        custom_theme.md_code = "\x1b[38;2;0;200;255m".to_string();

        let md_src = "# Custom Title\n\nInline `code_snippet` here.";
        let view = MarkdownView::new(md_src).with_theme(custom_theme);
        let lines = view.render(60);

        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("\x1b[38;2;255;0;128m# Custom Title")));
        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("\x1b[38;2;0;200;255mcode_snippet\x1b[0m")));
    }
}

use crate::ansi::{slice_by_column, visible_width};

pub const CURSOR_MARKER: &str = "\x1b_mehh:c\x07";

/// Trait implemented by interactive visual elements.
pub trait Component: Send + Sync {
    fn render(&self, width: usize) -> Vec<String>;
    fn handle_key(&mut self, _key: &str) -> bool {
        false
    }
    fn invalidate(&mut self) {}
}

/// Abstract terminal interface for rendering and testing.
pub trait Terminal: Send {
    fn size(&self) -> (u16, u16);
    fn write(&mut self, data: &str) -> std::io::Result<()>;
    fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()>;
}

/// Real crossterm-based terminal that manages raw mode and writes to stdout.
pub struct CrosstermTerminal {
    raw_mode_enabled: bool,
}

impl CrosstermTerminal {
    pub fn new() -> std::io::Result<Self> {
        crossterm::terminal::enable_raw_mode()?;
        let _ = crossterm::execute!(
            std::io::stdout(),
            crossterm::event::EnableBracketedPaste,
            crossterm::event::EnableMouseCapture
        );
        Ok(Self {
            raw_mode_enabled: true,
        })
    }
}

impl Drop for CrosstermTerminal {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.raw_mode_enabled {
            let _ = crossterm::execute!(
                std::io::stdout(),
                crossterm::event::DisableMouseCapture,
                crossterm::event::DisableBracketedPaste
            );
            let _ = crossterm::terminal::disable_raw_mode();
        }
    }
}

impl Terminal for CrosstermTerminal {
    fn size(&self) -> (u16, u16) {
        crossterm::terminal::size().unwrap_or((80, 24))
    }

    fn write(&mut self, data: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        use std::io::Write;
        std::io::stdout().write_all(data.as_bytes())
    }

    fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        use std::io::Write;
        std::io::stdout().flush()
    }
}

/// Headless terminal implementation with configurable dimensions and an output buffer.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MockTerminal {
    pub cols: u16,
    pub rows: u16,
    pub output: Vec<String>,
}

impl MockTerminal {
    pub fn new(cols: u16, rows: u16) -> Self {
        Self {
            cols,
            rows,
            output: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn full_output(&self) -> String {
        self.output.join("")
    }

    pub fn clear_output(&mut self) {
        self.output.clear();
    }
}

impl Terminal for MockTerminal {
    fn size(&self) -> (u16, u16) {
        (self.cols, self.rows)
    }

    fn write(&mut self, data: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        self.output.push(data.to_string());
        Ok(())
    }

    fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Layout options for compositing overlays (such as autocomplete popups).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct OverlayOptions {
    pub col: usize,
    pub row: usize,
    pub width: usize,
    pub max_height: Option<usize>,
}

/// Composite an overlay line onto a base line at column `col`, preserving styling.
pub fn composite_tui_line(
    base_line: &str,
    overlay_line: &str,
    col: usize,
    total_width: usize,
) -> String {
    let overlay_width = visible_width(overlay_line);
    let start_col = col.min(total_width);
    let after_start = (start_col + overlay_width).min(total_width);

    let before = slice_by_column(base_line, 0, start_col);
    let before_w = visible_width(&before);
    let before_pad = start_col.saturating_sub(before_w);

    let overlay_slice = if start_col + overlay_width > total_width {
        slice_by_column(overlay_line, 0, total_width.saturating_sub(start_col))
    } else {
        overlay_line.to_string()
    };
    let overlay_w = visible_width(&overlay_slice);
    let overlay_pad = total_width
        .saturating_sub(start_col)
        .min(overlay_width)
        .saturating_sub(overlay_w);

    let after = if after_start < total_width {
        slice_by_column(base_line, after_start, total_width)
    } else {
        String::new()
    };

    let mut res = String::new();
    res.push_str(&before);
    if before_pad > 0 {
        res.push_str(&" ".repeat(before_pad));
    }
    res.push_str("\x1b[0m");
    res.push_str(&overlay_slice);
    if overlay_pad > 0 {
        res.push_str(&" ".repeat(overlay_pad));
    }
    res.push_str("\x1b[0m");
    res.push_str(&after);

    let current_w = visible_width(&res);
    if current_w > total_width {
        slice_by_column(&res, 0, total_width)
    } else {
        res
    }
}

/// Differential terminal renderer.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct TuiRenderer {
    pub previous_lines: Vec<String>,
    pub previous_width: usize,
    pub cursor_row: usize,
}

impl TuiRenderer {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            previous_lines: Vec::new(),
            previous_width: 0,
            cursor_row: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.previous_lines.clear();
        self.previous_width = 0;
        self.cursor_row = 0;
    }

    pub fn render_frame(
        &mut self,
        terminal: &mut dyn Terminal,
        mut lines: Vec<String>,
        show_hardware_cursor: bool,
    ) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        let (cols, _rows) = terminal.size();
        let width = cols as usize;
        let width_changed = self.previous_width != 0 && self.previous_width != width;

        // Extract CURSOR_MARKER position
        let mut cursor_pos: Option<(usize, usize)> = None;
        for (row_idx, line) in lines.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if let Some(pos) = line.find(CURSOR_MARKER) {
                let col = visible_width(&line[..pos]);
                cursor_pos = Some((row_idx, col));
                *line = line.replace(CURSOR_MARKER, "");
                break;
            }
        }

        // Fake reverse-video cursor when hardware cursor is disabled
        if !show_hardware_cursor {
            if let Some((row, col)) = cursor_pos {
                if row < lines.len() {
                    let line = &lines[row];
                    let before = slice_by_column(line, 0, col);
                    let at = slice_by_column(line, col, col + 1);
                    let after = slice_by_column(line, col + 1, visible_width(line));
                    let cursor_char = if at.is_empty() { " " } else { &at };
                    lines[row] = format!("{before}\x1b[7m{cursor_char}\x1b[27m{after}");
                }
            }
        }

        let is_first = self.previous_lines.is_empty();
        let full_redraw = is_first || width_changed;

        let mut out = String::new();
        out.push_str("\x1b[?2026h"); // Synchronized output begin

        let mut final_cursor_row;

        if full_redraw {
            out.push('\r');
            for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
                out.push_str("\x1b[2K");
                out.push_str(line);
                if i + 1 < lines.len() {
                    out.push_str("\r\n");
                }
            }
            final_cursor_row = lines.len().saturating_sub(1);
        } else {
            let max_len = lines.len().max(self.previous_lines.len());
            let mut first_changed = None;
            let mut last_changed = None;

            for i in 0..max_len {
                let old_l = self.previous_lines.get(i).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                let new_l = lines.get(i).map(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                if old_l != new_l {
                    if first_changed.is_none() {
                        first_changed = Some(i);
                    }
                    last_changed = Some(i);
                }
            }

            if let (Some(first), Some(last)) = (first_changed, last_changed) {
                let diff = first as isize - self.cursor_row as isize;
                if diff < 0 {
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}A", -diff));
                } else if diff > 0 {
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}B", diff));
                }
                out.push('\r');

                let render_end = last.min(lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
                for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate().take(render_end + 1).skip(first) {
                    if i > first {
                        out.push_str("\r\n");
                    }
                    out.push_str("\x1b[2K");
                    out.push_str(line);
                }
                final_cursor_row = render_end;

                if self.previous_lines.len() > lines.len() {
                    if render_end < lines.len().saturating_sub(1) {
                        let move_down = lines.len() - 1 - render_end;
                        out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{move_down}B"));
                        final_cursor_row = lines.len() - 1;
                    }
                    let extra = self.previous_lines.len() - lines.len();
                    for _ in 0..extra {
                        out.push_str("\r\n\x1b[2K");
                    }
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{extra}A"));
                }
            } else {
                final_cursor_row = self.cursor_row;
            }
        }

        // Position hardware cursor if tracked
        if show_hardware_cursor {
            if let Some((target_row, target_col)) = cursor_pos {
                let target_row = target_row.min(lines.len().saturating_sub(1));
                let diff = target_row as isize - final_cursor_row as isize;
                if diff < 0 {
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}A", -diff));
                } else if diff > 0 {
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}B", diff));
                }
                out.push('\r');
                if target_col > 0 {
                    out.push_str(&format!("\x1b[{}C", target_col));
                }
                final_cursor_row = target_row;
            }
        }

        out.push_str("\x1b[?2026l"); // Synchronized output end

        terminal.write(&out)?;
        terminal.flush()?;

        self.previous_lines = lines;
        self.previous_width = width;
        self.cursor_row = final_cursor_row;

        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_differential_render_cursor_position_when_partial_lines_change() {
        let mut term = MockTerminal::new(40, 10);
        let mut renderer = TuiRenderer::new();

        // Initial 4 lines
        let frame1 = vec![
            "line0".to_string(),
            "line1".to_string(),
            "line2".to_string(),
            "line3".to_string(),
        ];
        renderer.render_frame(&mut term, frame1, false).unwrap();
        term.clear_output();

        // Frame 2: only line0 changes, with cursor at line0
        let frame2 = vec![
            format!("line0_new{CURSOR_MARKER}"),
            "line1".to_string(),
            "line2".to_string(),
            "line3".to_string(),
        ];
        renderer.render_frame(&mut term, frame2, true).unwrap();
        let out = term.full_output();

        // If cursor is at bottom (row 3) after re-rendering line0..line3, moving to line0 takes 3A.
        // But if renderer stops after line0, it should either advance to row 3 before moving 3A, or move 1A from row 1.
        // It must NOT emit an overshoot movement (e.g. moving up 3 from row 1).
        assert!(out.contains("line0_new"));
        assert!(!out.contains("\x1b[3A\r\x1b[2Kline0_new\r\n\x1b[3A"), "Out contained bad overshoot cursor move: {out:?}");
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
//! Theme definition, parsing, and color utilities for mehh TUI.

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Theme color tokens and styling configuration for TUI components.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct Theme {
    pub name: String,
    pub accent: String,
    pub border: String,
    pub border_accent: String,
    pub border_muted: String,
    pub success: String,
    pub error: String,
    pub warning: String,
    pub muted: String,
    pub dim: String,
    pub text: String,
    pub thinking_text: String,
    pub user_prefix: String,
    pub tool_pending: String,
    pub tool_success: String,
    pub tool_error: String,
    pub selected_bg: String,
    pub md_heading: String,
    pub md_code: String,
    pub md_quote: String,
    pub md_link: String,
}

impl Default for Theme {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::dark()
    }
}

/// Parse a 3- or 6-digit hex string into (r, g, b) bytes.
fn parse_hex_color(hex: &str) -> Option<(u8, u8, u8)> {
    let clean = hex.trim().strip_prefix('#').unwrap_or(hex.trim());
    if !clean.is_ascii() {
        return None;
    }
    if clean.len() == 6 {
        let r = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[0..2], 16).ok()?;
        let g = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[2..4], 16).ok()?;
        let b = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[4..6], 16).ok()?;
        Some((r, g, b))
    } else if clean.len() == 3 {
        let r = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[0..1], 16).ok()?;
        let g = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[1..2], 16).ok()?;
        let b = u8::from_str_radix(&clean[2..3], 16).ok()?;
        Some((r * 17, g * 17, b * 17))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

/// Convert a `#rrggbb` or `#rgb` hex string to an ANSI truecolor 24-bit foreground escape code.
pub fn hex_to_fg_ansi(hex: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let (r, g, b) = parse_hex_color(hex)?;
    Some(format!("\x1b[38;2;{r};{g};{b}m"))
}

/// Convert a `#rrggbb` or `#rgb` hex string to an ANSI truecolor 24-bit background escape code.
pub fn hex_to_bg_ansi(hex: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let (r, g, b) = parse_hex_color(hex)?;
    Some(format!("\x1b[48;2;{r};{g};{b}m"))
}

/// Resolve a color value string: converts hex colors to ANSI SGR escapes, or preserves raw ANSI escapes.
fn resolve_color_value(val: &str, is_bg: bool) -> String {
    let trimmed = val.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return String::new();
    }

    // If it's a valid hex code, convert it
    if let Some(ansi) = if is_bg {
        hex_to_bg_ansi(trimmed)
    } else {
        hex_to_fg_ansi(trimmed)
    } {
        return ansi;
    }

    // Common standard color name mapping
    match trimmed.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "black" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[40m" } else { "\x1b[30m" }).to_string(),
        "red" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[41m" } else { "\x1b[31m" }).to_string(),
        "green" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[42m" } else { "\x1b[32m" }).to_string(),
        "yellow" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[43m" } else { "\x1b[33m" }).to_string(),
        "blue" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[44m" } else { "\x1b[34m" }).to_string(),
        "magenta" | "violet" | "purple" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[45m" } else { "\x1b[35m" }).to_string(),
        "cyan" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[46m" } else { "\x1b[36m" }).to_string(),
        "white" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[47m" } else { "\x1b[37m" }).to_string(),
        "gray" | "grey" | "muted" => (if is_bg { "\x1b[100m" } else { "\x1b[90m" }).to_string(),
        // Raw ANSI escapes or unchanged fallback
        _ => trimmed.to_string(),
    }
}

impl Theme {
    /// Built-in dark theme with high-contrast ANSI SGR tokens.
    pub fn dark() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: "dark".to_string(),
            accent: "\x1b[36m".to_string(),
            border: "\x1b[34m".to_string(),
            border_accent: "\x1b[35m".to_string(),
            border_muted: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            success: "\x1b[32m".to_string(),
            error: "\x1b[31m".to_string(),
            warning: "\x1b[33m".to_string(),
            muted: "\x1b[90m".to_string(),
            dim: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            text: "\x1b[37m".to_string(),
            thinking_text: "\x1b[2;3m".to_string(),
            user_prefix: "\x1b[1;32m".to_string(),
            tool_pending: "\x1b[33m".to_string(),
            tool_success: "\x1b[32m".to_string(),
            tool_error: "\x1b[31m".to_string(),
            selected_bg: "\x1b[48;2;36;40;60m".to_string(),
            md_heading: "\x1b[1;36m".to_string(),
            md_code: "\x1b[33m".to_string(),
            md_quote: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            md_link: "\x1b[4;34m".to_string(),
        }
    }

    /// Built-in light theme.
    pub fn light() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: "light".to_string(),
            accent: "\x1b[34m".to_string(),
            border: "\x1b[34m".to_string(),
            border_accent: "\x1b[35m".to_string(),
            border_muted: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            success: "\x1b[32m".to_string(),
            error: "\x1b[31m".to_string(),
            warning: "\x1b[33m".to_string(),
            muted: "\x1b[90m".to_string(),
            dim: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            text: "\x1b[30m".to_string(),
            thinking_text: "\x1b[2;3m".to_string(),
            user_prefix: "\x1b[1;32m".to_string(),
            tool_pending: "\x1b[33m".to_string(),
            tool_success: "\x1b[32m".to_string(),
            tool_error: "\x1b[31m".to_string(),
            selected_bg: "\x1b[48;2;220;230;250m".to_string(),
            md_heading: "\x1b[1;34m".to_string(),
            md_code: "\x1b[35m".to_string(),
            md_quote: "\x1b[2m".to_string(),
            md_link: "\x1b[4;34m".to_string(),
        }
    }

    /// Resolve built-in theme by name ("light" -> light, else dark).
    pub fn from_name(name: &str) -> Self {
        match name.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "light" => Self::light(),
            _ => Self::dark(),
        }
    }

    /// Load a theme by name: checks `~/.mehh/agent/themes/<name>.json` (or a direct path),
    /// supporting `#rrggbb` hex colors converted to `\x1b[38;2;r;g;bm` or raw ANSI escapes,
    /// falling back to `Theme::from_name(...)` / `Theme::dark()`.
    pub fn load(name: Option<&str>) -> Self {
        let name_str = match name {
            Some(n) if !n.trim().is_empty() => n.trim(),
            _ => return Self::dark(),
        };

        let mut theme = Self::from_name(name_str);

        // Check direct path or ~/.mehh/agent/themes/<name>.json
        let direct_path = Path::new(name_str);
        let path = if direct_path.exists() {
            Some(direct_path.to_path_buf())
        } else {
            Self::theme_file_path(name_str)
        };

        let file_path = match path {
            Some(p) if p.exists() => p,
            _ => return theme,
        };

        let content = match std::fs::read_to_string(&file_path) {
            Ok(c) => c,
            Err(_) => return theme,
        };

        let val: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(&content) {
            Ok(v) => v,
            Err(_) => return theme,
        };

        Self::apply_json_value(&mut theme, &val);
        theme
    }

    fn theme_file_path(name: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        let home = std::env::var_os("USERPROFILE")
            .or_else(|| std::env::var_os("HOME"))
            .map(PathBuf::from)?;
        let filename = if name.ends_with(".json") {
            name.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("{name}.json")
        };
        Some(home.join(".mehh").join("agent").join("themes").join(filename))
    }

    fn apply_json_value(theme: &mut Theme, val: &serde_json::Value) {
        let obj = match val.as_object() {
            Some(o) => o,
            None => return,
        };

        if let Some(n) = obj.get("name").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
            theme.name = n.to_string();
        }

        let vars = obj.get("vars").and_then(|v| v.as_object());
        let colors = obj.get("colors").and_then(|v| v.as_object()).unwrap_or(obj);

        let get_color = |camel: &str, snake: &str, is_bg: bool| -> Option<String> {
            let mut raw = colors
                .get(camel)
                .or_else(|| colors.get(snake))
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())?;

            // If raw references a key in vars, resolve it
            if let Some(v_obj) = vars {
                if let Some(resolved) = v_obj.get(raw).and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                    raw = resolved;
                }
            }

            Some(resolve_color_value(raw, is_bg))
        };

        if let Some(c) = get_color("accent", "accent", false) { theme.accent = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("border", "border", false) { theme.border = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("borderAccent", "border_accent", false) { theme.border_accent = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("borderMuted", "border_muted", false) { theme.border_muted = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("success", "success", false) { theme.success = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("error", "error", false) { theme.error = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("warning", "warning", false) { theme.warning = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("muted", "muted", false) { theme.muted = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("dim", "dim", false) { theme.dim = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("text", "text", false) { theme.text = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("thinkingText", "thinking_text", false) { theme.thinking_text = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("userPrefix", "user_prefix", false) { theme.user_prefix = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("toolPending", "tool_pending", false) { theme.tool_pending = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("toolSuccess", "tool_success", false) { theme.tool_success = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("toolError", "tool_error", false) { theme.tool_error = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("selectedBg", "selected_bg", true) { theme.selected_bg = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("mdHeading", "md_heading", false) { theme.md_heading = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("mdCode", "md_code", false) { theme.md_code = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("mdQuote", "md_quote", false) { theme.md_quote = c; }
        if let Some(c) = get_color("mdLink", "md_link", false) { theme.md_link = c; }
    }

    /// Wrap `text` with `color_code` and reset `\x1b[0m` if `color_code` is non-empty.
    pub fn fg(&self, color_code: &str, text: &str) -> String {
        if color_code.is_empty() {
            text.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("{color_code}{text}\x1b[0m")
        }
    }

    /// Wrap `text` with `color_code` (background) and reset `\x1b[0m` if `color_code` is non-empty.
    pub fn bg(&self, color_code: &str, text: &str) -> String {
        if color_code.is_empty() {
            text.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("{color_code}{text}\x1b[0m")
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/mehh-tui/src/ansi.rs

use unicode_segmentation::UnicodeSegmentation;
use unicode_width::UnicodeWidthStr;

/// Return the length of the ANSI, OSC, or APC escape sequence starting at `pos`,
/// or 0 if none is found.
pub fn ansi_code_len(s: &str, pos: usize) -> usize {
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    if pos >= b.len() || b[pos] != 0x1b {
        return 0;
    }
    if pos + 1 >= b.len() {
        return 0;
    }

    match b[pos + 1] {
        // CSI sequence: ESC [ ... FinalByte (0x40..=0x7E)
        b'[' => {
            for (offset, &c) in b[pos + 2..].iter().enumerate() {
                if (0x40..=0x7e).contains(&c) {
                    return offset + 3;
                }
            }
            0
        }
        // OSC sequence: ESC ] ... BEL (0x07) or ST (ESC \)
        // APC sequence: ESC _ ... BEL (0x07) or ST (ESC \)
        // DCS sequence: ESC P ... BEL (0x07) or ST (ESC \)
        b']' | b'_' | b'P' => {
            let mut j = pos + 2;
            while j < b.len() {
                if b[j] == 0x07 {
                    return j + 1 - pos;
                }
                if b[j] == 0x1b && j + 1 < b.len() && b[j + 1] == b'\\' {
                    return j + 2 - pos;
                }
                j += 1;
            }
            0
        }
        // SS3 sequence: ESC O ...
        b'O' if pos + 2 < b.len() => 3,
        _ => 0,
    }
}

/// Strip CSI, OSC, and APC ANSI sequences from `s`.
pub fn strip_ansi(s: &str) -> String {
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    if !b.contains(&0x1b) {
        return s.to_string();
    }
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < b.len() {
        if b[i] == 0x1b {
            let len = ansi_code_len(s, i);
            if len > 0 {
                i += len;
            } else {
                result.push('\x1b');
                i += 1;
            }
        } else if let Some(rel_esc) = b[i..].iter().position(|&c| c == 0x1b) {
            result.push_str(&s[i..i + rel_esc]);
            i += rel_esc;
        } else {
            result.push_str(&s[i..]);
            break;
        }
    }
    result
}

/// Compute visible display width in terminal columns.
/// Strips ANSI/APC, treats tabs as 3 spaces, zero-width as 0.
pub fn visible_width(s: &str) -> usize {
    if s.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }

    // Printable ASCII fast-path without ANSI
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    let mut all_ascii = true;
    for &byte in b {
        if byte == 0x1b || byte == b'\t' || !(0x20..=0x7e).contains(&byte) {
            all_ascii = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if all_ascii {
        return s.len();
    }

    let clean: std::borrow::Cow<'_, str> = if b.contains(&0x1b) {
        std::borrow::Cow::Owned(strip_ansi(s))
    } else {
        std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(s)
    };
    let mut width = 0;
    for g in clean.graphemes(true) {
        if g == "\t" {
            width += 3;
        } else if g == "\r" || g == "\n" {
            // Newlines have zero display width
        } else {
            width += g.width();
        }
    }
    width
}

/// Track active ANSI SGR and OSC 8 styling attributes across line breaks.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct AnsiCodeTracker {
    pub bold: bool,
    pub dim: bool,
    pub italic: bool,
    pub underline: bool,
    pub blink: bool,
    pub inverse: bool,
    pub hidden: bool,
    pub strikethrough: bool,
    pub fg_color: Option<String>,
    pub bg_color: Option<String>,
    pub active_hyperlink: Option<(String, String, String)>, // (params, url, terminator)
}

impl AnsiCodeTracker {
    pub fn process(&mut self, code: &str) {
        // OSC 8 hyperlink: \x1b]8;params;url(\x07|\x1b\\)
        if code.starts_with("\x1b]8;") {
            let term = if code.ends_with("\x07") {
                "\x07".to_string()
            } else {
                "\x1b\\".to_string()
            };
            let body = if term == "\x07" {
                &code[4..code.len() - 1]
            } else {
                &code[4..code.len() - 2]
            };
            if let Some(idx) = body.find(';') {
                let params = &body[..idx];
                let url = &body[idx + 1..];
                if url.is_empty() {
                    self.active_hyperlink = None;
                } else {
                    self.active_hyperlink =
                        Some((params.to_string(), url.to_string(), term));
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        // CSI SGR code: \x1b[...m
        if code.starts_with("\x1b[") && code.ends_with('m') {
            let params = &code[2..code.len() - 1];
            if params.is_empty() || params == "0" {
                self.reset();
                return;
            }

            let parts: Vec<&str> = params.split(';').collect();
            let mut i = 0;
            while i < parts.len() {
                let num: u32 = parts[i].parse().unwrap_or(0);
                if (num == 38 || num == 48) && i + 2 < parts.len() && parts[i + 1] == "5" {
                    // 256 colors: 38;5;N or 48;5;N
                    let color = format!("{};5;{}", parts[i], parts[i + 2]);
                    if num == 38 {
                        self.fg_color = Some(color);
                    } else {
                        self.bg_color = Some(color);
                    }
                    i += 3;
                    continue;
                }
                if (num == 38 || num == 48) && i + 4 < parts.len() && parts[i + 1] == "2" {
                    // RGB colors: 38;2;R;G;B or 48;2;R;G;B
                    let color = format!(
                        "{};2;{};{};{}",
                        parts[i], parts[i + 2], parts[i + 3], parts[i + 4]
                    );
                    if num == 38 {
                        self.fg_color = Some(color);
                    } else {
                        self.bg_color = Some(color);
                    }
                    i += 5;
                    continue;
                }

                match num {
                    0 => self.reset(),
                    1 => self.bold = true,
                    2 => self.dim = true,
                    3 => self.italic = true,
                    4 => self.underline = true,
                    5 => self.blink = true,
                    7 => self.inverse = true,
                    8 => self.hidden = true,
                    9 => self.strikethrough = true,
                    21 => self.bold = false,
                    22 => {
                        self.bold = false;
                        self.dim = false;
                    }
                    23 => self.italic = false,
                    24 => self.underline = false,
                    25 => self.blink = false,
                    27 => self.inverse = false,
                    28 => self.hidden = false,
                    29 => self.strikethrough = false,
                    39 => self.fg_color = None,
                    49 => self.bg_color = None,
                    30..=37 | 90..=97 => self.fg_color = Some(num.to_string()),
                    40..=47 | 100..=107 => self.bg_color = Some(num.to_string()),
                    _ => {}
                }
                i += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self) {
        self.bold = false;
        self.dim = false;
        self.italic = false;
        self.underline = false;
        self.blink = false;
        self.inverse = false;
        self.hidden = false;
        self.strikethrough = false;
        self.fg_color = None;
        self.bg_color = None;
    }

    pub fn get_active_codes(&self) -> String {
        let mut codes = Vec::new();
        if self.bold {
            codes.push("1");
        }
        if self.dim {
            codes.push("2");
        }
        if self.italic {
            codes.push("3");
        }
        if self.underline {
            codes.push("4");
        }
        if self.blink {
            codes.push("5");
        }
        if self.inverse {
            codes.push("7");
        }
        if self.hidden {
            codes.push("8");
        }
        if self.strikethrough {
            codes.push("9");
        }
        if let Some(ref fg) = self.fg_color {
            codes.push(fg.as_str());
        }
        if let Some(ref bg) = self.bg_color {
            codes.push(bg.as_str());
        }

        let mut res = if codes.is_empty() {
            String::new()
        } else {
            format!("\x1b[{}m", codes.join(";"))
        };

        if let Some((ref params, ref url, ref term)) = self.active_hyperlink {
            res.push_str(&format!("\x1b]8;{params};{url}{term}"));
        }
        res
    }

    pub fn get_line_end_reset(&self) -> String {
        let mut res = String::new();
        if self.underline {
            res.push_str("\x1b[24m");
        }
        if self.strikethrough {
            res.push_str("\x1b[29m");
        }
        if let Some((_, _, ref term)) = self.active_hyperlink {
            res.push_str(&format!("\x1b]8;;{term}"));
        }
        res
    }

    pub fn has_active_codes(&self) -> bool {
        self.bold
            || self.dim
            || self.italic
            || self.underline
            || self.blink
            || self.inverse
            || self.hidden
            || self.strikethrough
            || self.fg_color.is_some()
            || self.bg_color.is_some()
            || self.active_hyperlink.is_some()
    }
}

/// Truncate text to fit within `max_width`, preserving active SGR styling,
/// appending `ellipsis` + `\x1b[0m`, and optionally padding with spaces to `max_width`.
pub fn truncate_to_width(s: &str, max_width: usize, ellipsis: &str, pad: bool) -> String {
    if max_width == 0 {
        return String::new();
    }
    if s.is_empty() {
        return if pad {
            " ".repeat(max_width)
        } else {
            String::new()
        };
    }

    let ell_width = visible_width(ellipsis);
    let s_width = visible_width(s);

    if s_width <= max_width {
        return if pad && s_width < max_width {
            format!("{s}{}", " ".repeat(max_width - s_width))
        } else {
            s.to_string()
        };
    }

    if ell_width >= max_width {
        let mut clipped = String::new();
        let mut cur = 0;
        for g in ellipsis.graphemes(true) {
            let w = g.width();
            if cur + w <= max_width {
                clipped.push_str(g);
                cur += w;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        let pad_len = if pad { max_width.saturating_sub(cur) } else { 0 };
        let reset = if s.contains('\x1b') { "\x1b[0m" } else { "" };
        return format!("{clipped}{reset}{}", " ".repeat(pad_len));
    }

    let target_width = max_width - ell_width;
    let mut result = String::new();
    let mut kept_width = 0;
    let mut tracker = AnsiCodeTracker::default();
    let mut pending_ansi = String::new();
    let mut i = 0;

    while i < s.len() {
        let code_len = ansi_code_len(s, i);
        if code_len > 0 {
            let code = &s[i..i + code_len];
            tracker.process(code);
            pending_ansi.push_str(code);
            i += code_len;
            continue;
        }

        let ch = s[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
        let next_i = i + ch.len_utf8();
        let grapheme = &s[i..next_i];
        let w = if grapheme == "\t" { 3 } else { grapheme.width() };

        if kept_width + w <= target_width {
            if !pending_ansi.is_empty() {
                result.push_str(&pending_ansi);
                pending_ansi.clear();
            }
            result.push_str(grapheme);
            kept_width += w;
            i = next_i;
        } else {
            break;
        }
    }

    let mut out = result;
    if tracker.has_active_codes() {
        if let Some((_, _, ref term)) = tracker.active_hyperlink {
            out.push_str(&format!("\x1b]8;;{term}"));
        }
        out.push_str("\x1b[0m");
        out.push_str(ellipsis);
        out.push_str("\x1b[0m");
    } else {
        out.push_str(ellipsis);
    }

    let total_cur_width = kept_width + ell_width;
    if pad && total_cur_width < max_width {
        out.push_str(&" ".repeat(max_width - total_cur_width));
    }

    out
}

/// Helper to test if a character is CJK for wrapping purposes.
fn is_cjk(c: char) -> bool {
    matches!(c,
        '\u{2E80}'..='\u{2EFF}' | // CJK Radicals Supplement
        '\u{2F00}'..='\u{2FDF}' | // Kangxi Radicals
        '\u{3000}'..='\u{303F}' | // CJK Symbols and Punctuation
        '\u{3040}'..='\u{309F}' | // Hiragana
        '\u{30A0}'..='\u{30FF}' | // Katakana
        '\u{3100}'..='\u{312F}' | // Bopomofo
        '\u{31A0}'..='\u{31BF}' | // Bopomofo Extended
        '\u{3400}'..='\u{4DBF}' | // CJK Unified Ideographs Extension A
        '\u{4E00}'..='\u{9FFF}' | // CJK Unified Ideographs
        '\u{AC00}'..='\u{D7AF}' | // Hangul Syllables
        '\u{F900}'..='\u{FAFF}'   // CJK Compatibility Ideographs
    )
}

/// Split text into tokens with attached ANSI codes.
/// CJK characters are split into single-character tokens.
fn split_tokens_with_ansi(text: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut tokens = Vec::new();
    let mut current = String::new();
    let mut pending_ansi = String::new();
    let mut current_is_space: Option<bool> = None;
    let mut i = 0;

    let flush = |tokens: &mut Vec<String>, current: &mut String, current_is_space: &mut Option<bool>| {
        if !current.is_empty() {
            tokens.push(std::mem::take(current));
            *current_is_space = None;
        }
    };

    while i < text.len() {
        let len = ansi_code_len(text, i);
        if len > 0 {
            pending_ansi.push_str(&text[i..i + len]);
            i += len;
            continue;
        }

        let ch = text[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
        let ch_len = ch.len_utf8();
        let seg = &text[i..i + ch_len];
        let is_space = ch == ' ' || ch == '\t';

        if !is_space && is_cjk(ch) {
            flush(&mut tokens, &mut current, &mut current_is_space);
            let mut tok = std::mem::take(&mut pending_ansi);
            tok.push_str(seg);
            tokens.push(tok);
            i += ch_len;
            continue;
        }

        if let Some(curr_space) = current_is_space {
            if curr_space != is_space {
                flush(&mut tokens, &mut current, &mut current_is_space);
            }
        }

        if !pending_ansi.is_empty() {
            current.push_str(&pending_ansi);
            pending_ansi.clear();
        }

        current_is_space = Some(is_space);
        current.push_str(seg);
        i += ch_len;
    }

    if !pending_ansi.is_empty() {
        if !current.is_empty() {
            current.push_str(&pending_ansi);
        } else if let Some(last) = tokens.last_mut() {
            last.push_str(&pending_ansi);
        } else {
            current.push_str(&pending_ansi);
        }
    }

    flush(&mut tokens, &mut current, &mut current_is_space);
    tokens
}

fn break_long_word(word: &str, width: usize, tracker: &mut AnsiCodeTracker) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    let mut current_line = tracker.get_active_codes();
    let mut current_w = 0;
    let mut i = 0;

    while i < word.len() {
        let code_len = ansi_code_len(word, i);
        if code_len > 0 {
            let code = &word[i..i + code_len];
            tracker.process(code);
            current_line.push_str(code);
            i += code_len;
            continue;
        }

        let ch = word[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
        let ch_len = ch.len_utf8();
        let seg = &word[i..i + ch_len];
        let w = if seg == "\t" { 3 } else { seg.width() };

        if current_w + w > width && current_w > 0 {
            let reset = tracker.get_line_end_reset();
            if !reset.is_empty() {
                current_line.push_str(&reset);
            }
            lines.push(current_line);
            current_line = tracker.get_active_codes();
            current_w = 0;
        }

        current_line.push_str(seg);
        current_w += w;
        i += ch_len;
    }

    if !current_line.is_empty() {
        lines.push(current_line);
    }

    lines
}

fn wrap_single_line(line: &str, width: usize, tracker: &mut AnsiCodeTracker) -> Vec<String> {
    if line.is_empty() {
        return vec![String::new()];
    }

    let vis_len = visible_width(line);
    if vis_len <= width {
        // Update tracker with line codes
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < line.len() {
            let len = ansi_code_len(line, i);
            if len > 0 {
                tracker.process(&line[i..i + len]);
                i += len;
            } else {
                let ch = line[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
                i += ch.len_utf8();
            }
        }
        return vec![line.to_string()];
    }

    let mut wrapped = Vec::new();
    let tokens = split_tokens_with_ansi(line);
    let mut current_line = tracker.get_active_codes();
    let mut current_len = 0;

    for token in tokens {
        let token_vis_len = visible_width(&token);
        let is_space = strip_ansi(&token).chars().all(|c| c == ' ' || c == '\t');

        if token_vis_len > width && !is_space {
            if current_len > 0 {
                let trimmed = current_line.trim_end().to_string();
                let reset = tracker.get_line_end_reset();
                let mut out = trimmed;
                if !reset.is_empty() {
                    out.push_str(&reset);
                }
                wrapped.push(out);
                current_line = tracker.get_active_codes();
                current_len = 0;
            }

            let broken = break_long_word(&token, width, tracker);
            for (idx, b_line) in broken.iter().enumerate() {
                if idx + 1 < broken.len() {
                    wrapped.push(b_line.clone());
                } else {
                    current_line = b_line.clone();
                    current_len = visible_width(&current_line);
                }
            }
            continue;
        }

        if current_len + token_vis_len > width && current_len > 0 {
            let trimmed = current_line.trim_end().to_string();
            let reset = tracker.get_line_end_reset();
            let mut out = trimmed;
            if !reset.is_empty() {
                out.push_str(&reset);
            }
            wrapped.push(out);

            if is_space {
                current_line = tracker.get_active_codes();
                current_len = 0;
            } else {
                current_line = format!("{}{token}", tracker.get_active_codes());
                current_len = token_vis_len;
            }
        } else {
            current_line.push_str(&token);
            current_len += token_vis_len;
        }

        // Update tracker with tokens
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < token.len() {
            let len = ansi_code_len(&token, i);
            if len > 0 {
                tracker.process(&token[i..i + len]);
                i += len;
            } else {
                let ch = token[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
                i += ch.len_utf8();
            }
        }
    }

    if !current_line.is_empty() {
        wrapped.push(current_line);
    }

    if wrapped.is_empty() {
        vec![String::new()]
    } else {
        wrapped.into_iter().map(|l| l.trim_end().to_string()).collect()
    }
}

/// Wrap lines at word boundaries (breaking long words if wider than `width`),
/// tracking active SGR and OSC 8 across wrapped lines.
pub fn wrap_text_with_ansi(text: &str, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
    if text.is_empty() {
        return vec![String::new()];
    }
    if width == 0 {
        return vec![text.to_string()];
    }

    let input_lines: Vec<&str> = text.split("\r\n").flat_map(|s| s.split('\n')).flat_map(|s| s.split('\r')).collect();
    let mut result = Vec::new();
    let mut tracker = AnsiCodeTracker::default();

    for line in input_lines {
        let wrapped = wrap_single_line(line, width, &mut tracker);
        for w in wrapped {
            result.push(w);
        }
    }

    if result.is_empty() {
        vec![String::new()]
    } else {
        result
    }
}

/// Extract visible column slice `[start_col, end_col)` while preserving SGR styles.
pub fn slice_by_column(line: &str, start_col: usize, end_col: usize) -> String {
    if end_col <= start_col {
        return String::new();
    }

    let mut result = String::new();
    let mut current_col = 0;
    let mut pending_ansi = String::new();
    let mut tracker = AnsiCodeTracker::default();
    let mut i = 0;

    while i < line.len() {
        let code_len = ansi_code_len(line, i);
        if code_len > 0 {
            let code = &line[i..i + code_len];
            tracker.process(code);

            if current_col >= start_col && current_col < end_col {
                if !pending_ansi.is_empty() {
                    result.push_str(&pending_ansi);
                    pending_ansi.clear();
                }
                result.push_str(code);
            } else if current_col < start_col && !code.starts_with("\x1b_") {
                pending_ansi.push_str(code);
            }
            i += code_len;
            continue;
        }

        let ch = line[i..].chars().next().unwrap();
        let ch_len = ch.len_utf8();
        let seg = &line[i..i + ch_len];
        let w = if seg == "\t" { 3 } else { seg.width() };

        let in_range = current_col >= start_col && current_col < end_col;
        if in_range {
            if !pending_ansi.is_empty() {
                result.push_str(&pending_ansi);
                pending_ansi.clear();
            }
            result.push_str(seg);
        }

        current_col += w;
        i += ch_len;

        if current_col >= end_col {
            break;
        }
    }

    if tracker.has_active_codes() && !result.is_empty() {
        result.push_str("\x1b[0m");
    }

    result
}

use std::collections::{BTreeSet, HashMap};
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, KeyModifiers};

/// Map a crossterm `KeyEvent` into a canonical lowercase key identifier string.
/// E.g. `"ctrl+c"`, `"shift+enter"`, `"alt+left"`, `"enter"`, `"escape"`,
/// `"backspace"`, `"delete"`, `"tab"`, `"backtab"`, `"up"`, `"down"`,
/// `"left"`, `"right"`, `"home"`, `"end"`, `"pageup"`, `"pagedown"`.
pub fn normalize_crossterm_key(event: KeyEvent) -> Option<String> {
    if event.kind == KeyEventKind::Release {
        return None;
    }

    let has_ctrl = event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let has_alt = event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::ALT);
    let has_shift = event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::SHIFT);
    let has_super = event.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::SUPER);

    let make_mod_str = |base: &str, include_shift: bool| -> String {
        let mut parts = Vec::new();
        if has_ctrl {
            parts.push("ctrl");
        }
        if has_alt {
            parts.push("alt");
        }
        if has_shift && include_shift {
            parts.push("shift");
        }
        if has_super {
            parts.push("super");
        }
        parts.push(base);
        parts.join("+")
    };

    match event.code {
        KeyCode::Char(c) => {
            if has_ctrl || has_alt || has_super {
                let lower = c.to_ascii_lowercase().to_string();
                Some(make_mod_str(&lower, has_shift || c.is_ascii_uppercase()))
            } else {
                Some(c.to_string())
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Enter => Some(make_mod_str("enter", true)),
        KeyCode::Tab => {
            if has_shift {
                Some("backtab".to_string())
            } else {
                Some(make_mod_str("tab", false))
            }
        }
        KeyCode::BackTab => Some("backtab".to_string()),
        KeyCode::Esc => Some(make_mod_str("escape", true)),
        KeyCode::Backspace => Some(make_mod_str("backspace", true)),
        KeyCode::Delete => Some(make_mod_str("delete", true)),
        KeyCode::Left => Some(make_mod_str("left", true)),
        KeyCode::Right => Some(make_mod_str("right", true)),
        KeyCode::Up => Some(make_mod_str("up", true)),
        KeyCode::Down => Some(make_mod_str("down", true)),
        KeyCode::Home => Some(make_mod_str("home", true)),
        KeyCode::End => Some(make_mod_str("end", true)),
        KeyCode::PageUp => Some(make_mod_str("pageup", true)),
        KeyCode::PageDown => Some(make_mod_str("pagedown", true)),
        KeyCode::F(n) => {
            let f = format!("f{n}");
            Some(make_mod_str(&f, true))
        }
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn canonicalize_key_spec(spec: &str) -> (BTreeSet<String>, String) {
    if spec == " " {
        return (BTreeSet::new(), " ".to_string());
    }
    let trimmed = spec.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return (BTreeSet::new(), String::new());
    }

    let (raw_mods, raw_base): (Vec<&str>, &str) = if trimmed == "+" {
        (Vec::new(), "+")
    } else if let Some(prefix) = trimmed.strip_suffix("++") {
        (prefix.split('+').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).collect(), "+")
    } else if let Some(idx) = trimmed.rfind('+') {
        let prefix = &trimmed[..idx];
        let base = &trimmed[idx + 1..];
        (prefix.split('+').filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).collect(), base)
    } else {
        (Vec::new(), trimmed)
    };

    let mut modifiers = BTreeSet::new();
    for m in raw_mods {
        match m.to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "ctrl" | "control" => {
                modifiers.insert("ctrl".to_string());
            }
            "alt" | "opt" | "option" => {
                modifiers.insert("alt".to_string());
            }
            "shift" => {
                modifiers.insert("shift".to_string());
            }
            "super" | "cmd" | "command" | "win" | "meta" => {
                modifiers.insert("super".to_string());
            }
            other => {
                modifiers.insert(other.to_string());
            }
        }
    }

    let lower_base = raw_base.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let base = match lower_base.as_str() {
        "esc" => "escape",
        "return" => "enter",
        "space" => " ",
        "plus" => "+",
        "backtab" => {
            modifiers.insert("shift".to_string());
            "tab"
        }
        "page_up" => "pageup",
        "page_down" => "pagedown",
        _ => raw_base,
    };

    (modifiers, base.to_string())
}

/// Check if a key string matches a binding pattern, accounting for aliases
/// and modifier ordering.
pub fn matches_key(key: &str, pattern: &str) -> bool {
    if key.eq_ignore_ascii_case(pattern) {
        return true;
    }

    let (k_mods, k_base) = canonicalize_key_spec(key);
    let (p_mods, p_base) = canonicalize_key_spec(pattern);

    k_mods == p_mods && k_base == p_base
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum TuiAction {
    CursorLeft,
    CursorRight,
    CursorUp,
    CursorDown,
    WordLeft,
    WordRight,
    LineStart,
    LineEnd,
    Submit,
    Newline,
    Backspace,
    Delete,
    DeleteWordBackward,
    DeleteWordForward,
    KillToEnd,
    KillToStart,
    Yank,
    YankPop,
    Undo,
    HistoryPrev,
    HistoryNext,
    TabComplete,
    Interrupt,
    ClearOrExit,
    ScrollUp,
    ScrollDown,
    ScrollBottom,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Keybindings {
    bindings: HashMap<TuiAction, Vec<String>>,
}

impl Default for Keybindings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::default_bindings()
    }
}

impl Keybindings {
    pub fn default_bindings() -> Self {
        let mut map = HashMap::new();
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::CursorLeft,
            vec!["left".into(), "ctrl+b".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::CursorRight,
            vec!["right".into(), "ctrl+f".into()],
        );
        map.insert(TuiAction::CursorUp, vec!["up".into()]);
        map.insert(TuiAction::CursorDown, vec!["down".into()]);
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::WordLeft,
            vec!["alt+left".into(), "ctrl+left".into(), "alt+b".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::WordRight,
            vec!["alt+right".into(), "ctrl+right".into(), "alt+f".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::LineStart,
            vec!["home".into(), "ctrl+a".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::LineEnd,
            vec!["end".into(), "ctrl+e".into()],
        );
        map.insert(TuiAction::Submit, vec!["enter".into()]);
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::Newline,
            vec!["shift+enter".into(), "ctrl+j".into(), "alt+enter".into()],
        );
        map.insert(TuiAction::Backspace, vec!["backspace".into()]);
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::Delete,
            vec!["delete".into(), "ctrl+d".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::DeleteWordBackward,
            vec!["ctrl+w".into(), "alt+backspace".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::DeleteWordForward,
            vec!["alt+d".into(), "alt+delete".into()],
        );
        map.insert(TuiAction::KillToEnd, vec!["ctrl+k".into()]);
        map.insert(TuiAction::KillToStart, vec!["ctrl+u".into()]);
        map.insert(TuiAction::Yank, vec!["ctrl+y".into()]);
        map.insert(TuiAction::YankPop, vec!["alt+y".into()]);
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::Undo,
            vec!["ctrl+z".into(), "ctrl+-".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::HistoryPrev,
            vec!["up".into(), "alt+p".into(), "ctrl+p".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::HistoryNext,
            vec!["down".into(), "alt+n".into(), "ctrl+n".into()],
        );
        map.insert(TuiAction::TabComplete, vec!["tab".into()]);
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::Interrupt,
            vec!["escape".into(), "ctrl+c".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::ClearOrExit,
            vec!["ctrl+c".into(), "ctrl+d".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::ScrollUp,
            vec!["pageup".into(), "shift+up".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::ScrollDown,
            vec!["pagedown".into(), "shift+down".into()],
        );
        map.insert(
            TuiAction::ScrollBottom,
            vec!["ctrl+end".into(), "shift+end".into()],
        );

        Self { bindings: map }
    }

    pub fn matches(&self, key: &str, action: TuiAction) -> bool {
        if let Some(keys) = self.bindings.get(&action) {
            keys.iter().any(|pattern| matches_key(key, pattern))
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    pub fn bind(&mut self, action: TuiAction, pattern: impl Into<String>) {
        self.bindings.entry(action).or_default().push(pattern.into());
    }

    pub fn set_bindings(&mut self, action: TuiAction, patterns: Vec<String>) {
        self.bindings.insert(action, patterns);
    }

    pub fn get_bindings(&self, action: TuiAction) -> Option<&[String]> {
        self.bindings.get(&action).map(|v| v.as_slice())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_normalize_crossterm_key_ctrl_shift_lowercase_char() {
        // When user presses Ctrl+Shift+P, crossterm may deliver KeyCode::Char('p') with CONTROL | SHIFT.
        let ev = KeyEvent::new(
            KeyCode::Char('p'),
            KeyModifiers::CONTROL | KeyModifiers::SHIFT,
        );
        assert_eq!(
            normalize_crossterm_key(ev),
            Some("ctrl+shift+p".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_canonicalize_plus_key_spec() {
        assert!(matches_key("+", "+"));
        assert!(matches_key("ctrl++", "ctrl++"));
        assert!(matches_key("ctrl+shift++", "ctrl+shift++"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_matches_key_space_alias() {
        assert!(matches_key(" ", "space"));
        assert!(matches_key("space", " "));
    }
}

//! Fuzzy matching and filtering utilities ported from TypeScript `packages/tui/src/fuzzy.ts`.
//! Matches if all query characters appear in order (not necessarily consecutive).
//! Lower score = better match.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct FuzzyMatch {
    pub matches: bool,
    pub score: f64,
}

fn score_positions(text_chars: &[char], positions: &[usize]) -> f64 {
    let mut score = 0.0;
    let mut last_match_index: isize = -1;
    let mut consecutive_matches: usize = 0;

    for &i in positions {
        // Reward consecutive matches
        if last_match_index == i as isize - 1 {
            consecutive_matches += 1;
            score -= (consecutive_matches as f64) * 5.0;
        } else {
            consecutive_matches = 0;
            // Penalize gaps
            if last_match_index >= 0 {
                score += ((i as isize - last_match_index - 1) as f64) * 2.0;
            }
        }

        // Reward word boundary matches
        if i == 0
            || (i > 0
                && matches!(
                    text_chars[i - 1],
                    ' ' | '\t' | '\n' | '\r' | '-' | '_' | '.' | '/' | ':'
                ))
        {
            score -= 10.0;
        }

        // Slight penalty for later matches
        score += (i as f64) * 0.1;

        last_match_index = i as isize;
    }

    score
}

fn match_query(normalized_query: &str, text_lower: &str) -> FuzzyMatch {
    if normalized_query.is_empty() {
        return FuzzyMatch {
            matches: true,
            score: 0.0,
        };
    }

    let query_chars: Vec<char> = normalized_query.chars().collect();
    let text_chars: Vec<char> = text_lower.chars().collect();

    if query_chars.len() > text_chars.len() {
        return FuzzyMatch {
            matches: false,
            score: 0.0,
        };
    }

    // Greedy alignment: each query character at its first occurrence after the previous one.
    let mut positions = Vec::with_capacity(query_chars.len());
    let mut last_match_index: isize = -1;

    for &qc in &query_chars {
        let start_idx = (last_match_index + 1) as usize;
        let mut found = None;
        for (offset, &tc) in text_chars[start_idx..].iter().enumerate() {
            if tc == qc {
                found = Some(start_idx + offset);
                break;
            }
        }

        match found {
            Some(idx) => {
                positions.push(idx);
                last_match_index = idx as isize;
            }
            None => {
                return FuzzyMatch {
                    matches: false,
                    score: 0.0,
                };
            }
        }
    }

    let mut score = score_positions(&text_chars, &positions);

    // The greedy alignment can scatter the query over an earlier, unrelated part of the text.
    // Also score every contiguous occurrence and keep the best alignment.
    if !query_chars.is_empty() && text_chars.len() >= query_chars.len() {
        let q_len = query_chars.len();
        for (start, window) in text_chars.windows(q_len).enumerate() {
            if window == query_chars.as_slice() {
                let mut contig_positions = Vec::with_capacity(q_len);
                for k in 0..q_len {
                    contig_positions.push(start + k);
                }
                let contig_score = score_positions(&text_chars, &contig_positions);
                if contig_score < score {
                    score = contig_score;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if normalized_query == text_lower {
        score -= 100.0;
    }

    FuzzyMatch {
        matches: true,
        score,
    }
}

fn split_letters_digits(s: &str) -> Option<(&str, &str)> {
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let letter_count = bytes.iter().take_while(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase()).count();
    if letter_count == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let digit_count = bytes[letter_count..]
        .iter()
        .take_while(|b| b.is_ascii_digit())
        .count();
    if digit_count > 0 && letter_count + digit_count == bytes.len() {
        Some((&s[..letter_count], &s[letter_count..]))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn split_digits_letters(s: &str) -> Option<(&str, &str)> {
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let digit_count = bytes.iter().take_while(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()).count();
    if digit_count == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    let letter_count = bytes[digit_count..]
        .iter()
        .take_while(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase())
        .count();
    if letter_count > 0 && digit_count + letter_count == bytes.len() {
        Some((&s[..digit_count], &s[digit_count..]))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

/// Matches if all characters in `query` appear in order in `text`.
/// Lower score = better match.
pub fn fuzzy_match(query: &str, text: &str) -> FuzzyMatch {
    let query_lower = query.to_lowercase();
    let text_lower = text.to_lowercase();

    let primary_match = match_query(&query_lower, &text_lower);
    if primary_match.matches {
        return primary_match;
    }

    let swapped_query = if let Some((letters, digits)) = split_letters_digits(&query_lower) {
        Some(format!("{}{}", digits, letters))
    } else if let Some((digits, letters)) = split_digits_letters(&query_lower) {
        Some(format!("{}{}", letters, digits))
    } else {
        None
    };

    let swapped_query = match swapped_query {
        Some(q) => q,
        None => return primary_match,
    };

    let swapped_match = match_query(&swapped_query, &text_lower);
    if !swapped_match.matches {
        return primary_match;
    }

    FuzzyMatch {
        matches: true,
        score: swapped_match.score + 5.0,
    }
}

/// Filter and sort items by fuzzy match quality, returning matched item indices and references.
/// Supports whitespace- and slash-separated tokens: all tokens must match.
pub fn fuzzy_filter_indexed<'a, T, S: AsRef<str>>(
    items: &'a [T],
    query: &str,
    get_text: impl Fn(&T) -> S,
) -> Vec<(usize, &'a T)> {
    let query_trimmed = query.trim();
    if query_trimmed.is_empty() {
        return items.iter().enumerate().collect();
    }

    let tokens: Vec<&str> = query_trimmed
        .split(|c: char| c.is_whitespace() || c == '/')
        .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
        .collect();

    if tokens.is_empty() {
        return items.iter().enumerate().collect();
    }

    let mut results: Vec<(usize, &'a T, f64)> = Vec::new();

    for (idx, item) in items.iter().enumerate() {
        let text = get_text(item);
        let text_ref = text.as_ref();
        let mut total_score = 0.0;
        let mut all_match = true;

        for &token in &tokens {
            let m = fuzzy_match(token, text_ref);
            if m.matches {
                total_score += m.score;
            } else {
                all_match = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if all_match {
            results.push((idx, item, total_score));
        }
    }

    results.sort_by(|a, b| {
        a.2.partial_cmp(&b.2)
            .unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
    });

    results.into_iter().map(|(idx, item, _)| (idx, item)).collect()
}

/// Filter and sort items by fuzzy match quality (best matches first).
/// Supports whitespace- and slash-separated tokens: all tokens must match.
pub fn fuzzy_filter<'a, T, S: AsRef<str>>(
    items: &'a [T],
    query: &str,
    get_text: impl Fn(&T) -> S,
) -> Vec<&'a T> {
    fuzzy_filter_indexed(items, query, get_text)
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(_, item)| item)
        .collect()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_empty_query_matches_everything_with_zero_score() {
        let result = fuzzy_match("", "anything");
        assert!(result.matches);
        assert_eq!(result.score, 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_query_longer_than_text_does_not_match() {
        let result = fuzzy_match("longquery", "short");
        assert!(!result.matches);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_exact_match_has_good_score() {
        let result = fuzzy_match("test", "test");
        assert!(result.matches);
        assert!(result.score < 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_characters_must_appear_in_order() {
        let match_in_order = fuzzy_match("abc", "aXbXc");
        assert!(match_in_order.matches);

        let match_out_of_order = fuzzy_match("abc", "cba");
        assert!(!match_out_of_order.matches);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_case_insensitive_matching() {
        let result = fuzzy_match("ABC", "abc");
        assert!(result.matches);

        let result2 = fuzzy_match("abc", "ABC");
        assert!(result2.matches);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_consecutive_matches_score_better_than_scattered_matches() {
        let consecutive = fuzzy_match("foo", "foobar");
        let scattered = fuzzy_match("foo", "f_o_o_bar");

        assert!(consecutive.matches);
        assert!(scattered.matches);
        assert!(consecutive.score < scattered.score);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_word_boundary_matches_score_better() {
        let at_boundary = fuzzy_match("fb", "foo-bar");
        let not_at_boundary = fuzzy_match("fb", "afbx");

        assert!(at_boundary.matches);
        assert!(not_at_boundary.matches);
        assert!(at_boundary.score < not_at_boundary.score);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_scores_contiguous_occurrence_instead_of_earlier_scattered_alignment() {
        let opus = fuzzy_match("opus", "openrouter openrouter/anthropic/claude-opus-4.1");
        let sonnet = fuzzy_match("opus", "openrouter openrouter/anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5");
        let gpt_oss = fuzzy_match("opus", "openrouter openrouter/openai/gpt-oss-120b");

        assert!(opus.score < sonnet.score);
        assert!(opus.score < gpt_oss.score);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_uses_best_contiguous_occurrence_not_first() {
        let embedded_first = fuzzy_match("opus", "xxopus opus");
        let boundary_only = fuzzy_match("opus", "xxxxxx opus");

        assert!((embedded_first.score - boundary_only.score).abs() < 1e-6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_matches_swapped_alpha_numeric_tokens() {
        let result = fuzzy_match("codex52", "gpt-5.2-codex");
        assert!(result.matches);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_empty_query_returns_all_items_unchanged() {
        let items = vec!["apple", "banana", "cherry"];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "", |x| *x);
        assert_eq!(result, items.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_filters_out_non_matching_items() {
        let items = vec!["apple", "banana", "cherry"];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "an", |x| *x);
        assert!(result.contains(&&"banana"));
        assert!(!result.contains(&&"apple"));
        assert!(!result.contains(&&"cherry"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_sorts_results_by_match_quality() {
        let items = vec!["a_p_p", "app", "application"];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "app", |x| *x);
        assert_eq!(*result[0], "app");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_prioritizes_exact_matches_over_longer_prefix_matches() {
        let items = vec!["clone", "cl"];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "cl", |x| *x);
        assert_eq!(result, vec![&"cl", &"clone"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_ranks_contiguous_above_shared_provider_prefix() {
        let items = vec![
            "radius radius/gpt-5.4 radius gpt-5.4 GPT 5.4",
            "radius radius/agentic radius agentic Agentic",
            "radius radius/gpt-6-sol radius gpt-6-sol GPT 6 Sol",
            "radius radius/gpt-6-astra radius gpt-6-astra GPT 6 Astra",
        ];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "astra", |x| *x);
        assert_eq!(*result[0], items[3]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_matches_slash_separated_queries_against_reordered_text() {
        struct Model {
            id: &'static str,
            provider: &'static str,
        }
        let items = vec![Model {
            id: "gpt-5.5",
            provider: "openai-codex",
        }];
        let result = fuzzy_filter(&items, "openai-codex/gpt-5.5", |m| {
            format!("{} {}", m.id, m.provider)
        });
        assert_eq!(result.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(result[0].id, "gpt-5.5");
    }
}
