use pulldown_cmark::{CodeBlockKind, Event, HeadingLevel, Options, Parser, Tag, TagEnd};

use crate::ansi::{visible_width, wrap_text_with_ansi};
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crate::tui::Component;

/// Formatted markdown viewer component using `pulldown_cmark`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MarkdownView {
    pub source: String,
    pub theme: Theme,
}

impl MarkdownView {
    pub fn new(source: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            source: source.into(),
            theme: Theme::dark(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_theme(mut self, theme: Theme) -> Self {
        self.theme = theme;
        self
    }
}

fn sanitize_markdown_text(s: &str, allow_newlines: bool) -> String {
    let stripped = crate::ansi::strip_ansi(s);
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(stripped.len());
    for ch in stripped.chars() {
        match ch {
            '\n' if allow_newlines => out.push(ch),
            '\r' if allow_newlines => {}
            '\t' => out.push_str("   "),
            c if (c as u32) < 0x20 || c == '\x7f' || ('\u{0080}'..='\u{009f}').contains(&c) => {}
            c => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

fn sanitize_osc8_url(raw: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let clean = sanitize_markdown_text(raw, false);
    let trimmed = clean.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let lower = trimmed.to_ascii_lowercase();
    if lower.starts_with("javascript:") || lower.starts_with("data:") {
        return None;
    }
    Some(trimmed.to_string())
}

fn format_markdown_table(
    rows: &[Vec<String>],
    has_header: bool,
    max_width: usize,
    border_color: &str,
) -> Vec<String> {
    if rows.is_empty() || max_width == 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let col_count = rows.iter().map(|r| r.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
    if col_count == 0 {
        return Vec::new();
    }

    let mut col_widths = vec![3usize; col_count];
    for row in rows {
        for (c, cell) in row.iter().enumerate() {
            col_widths[c] = col_widths[c].max(visible_width(cell));
        }
    }

    let overhead = col_count * 3 + 1;
    if max_width > overhead {
        let avail_cells = max_width - overhead;
        while col_widths.iter().sum::<usize>() > avail_cells {
            if let Some((idx, _)) = col_widths
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .filter(|(_, &w)| w > 1)
                .max_by_key(|(_, &w)| w)
            {
                col_widths[idx] -= 1;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    } else {
        col_widths.fill(1);
    }

    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for (r_idx, row) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let mut line = format!("{border_color}|\x1b[0m");
        for (c_idx, &col_w) in col_widths.iter().enumerate() {
            let cell = row.get(c_idx).map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
            let formatted = crate::ansi::truncate_to_width(cell, col_w, "…", true);
            line.push(' ');
            if has_header && r_idx == 0 {
                line.push_str("\x1b[1m");
                line.push_str(&formatted);
                line.push_str("\x1b[0m");
            } else {
                line.push_str(&formatted);
            }
            line.push_str(&format!(" {border_color}|\x1b[0m"));
        }
        lines.push(line);

        if has_header && r_idx == 0 {
            let mut sep = format!("{border_color}|");
            for &col_w in &col_widths {
                sep.push_str(&"─".repeat(col_w + 2));
                sep.push('|');
            }
            sep.push_str("\x1b[0m");
            lines.push(sep);
        }
    }
    lines
}

fn push_blockquote_lines(
    target: &mut Vec<String>,
    lines: Vec<String>,
    quote_depth: usize,
    quote_color: &str,
) {
    if quote_depth == 0 {
        target.extend(lines);
        return;
    }
    let quote_prefix = format!("{quote_color}│\x1b[0m ").repeat(quote_depth);
    for l in lines {
        target.push(format!("{quote_prefix}{l}"));
    }
}

impl Component for MarkdownView {
    fn render(&self, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        if width == 0 {
            return Vec::new();
        }

        let mut options = Options::empty();
        options.insert(Options::ENABLE_STRIKETHROUGH);
        options.insert(Options::ENABLE_TABLES);
        options.insert(Options::ENABLE_TASKLISTS);

        let clean_source = crate::ansi::strip_ansi(&self.source);
        let parser = Parser::new_ext(&clean_source, options);

        let mut output_lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let mut current_line = String::new();

        let mut in_code_block = false;
        let mut code_block_lang = String::new();
        let mut code_block_buf = String::new();

        let mut in_table = false;
        let mut table_has_header = false;
        let mut table_rows: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::new();
        let mut table_current_row: Vec<String> = Vec::new();

        let mut blockquote_depth = 0usize;
        let mut list_stack: Vec<Option<u64>> = Vec::new();
        let mut link_osc8_stack: Vec<bool> = Vec::new();
        let mut active_heading_style: Option<String> = None;

        let quote_color = if !self.theme.md_quote.is_empty() {
            &self.theme.md_quote
        } else {
            "\x1b[2m"
        };
        let border_color = if !self.theme.border_muted.is_empty() {
            &self.theme.border_muted
        } else {
            "\x1b[2m"
        };
        let code_color = if !self.theme.md_code.is_empty() {
            &self.theme.md_code
        } else {
            "\x1b[33m"
        };

        let flush_current_line =
            |current: &mut String, lines: &mut Vec<String>, quote_depth: usize| {
                if !current.is_empty() {
                    let line_content = std::mem::take(current);
                    push_blockquote_lines(lines, vec![line_content], quote_depth, quote_color);
                }
            };

        for event in parser {
            match event {
                Event::Start(Tag::Heading { level, .. }) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    let prefix = match level {
                        HeadingLevel::H1 => "# ",
                        HeadingLevel::H2 => "## ",
                        HeadingLevel::H3 => "### ",
                        HeadingLevel::H4 => "#### ",
                        HeadingLevel::H5 => "##### ",
                        HeadingLevel::H6 => "###### ",
                    };
                    let heading_style = if !self.theme.md_heading.is_empty() {
                        self.theme.md_heading.clone()
                    } else {
                        "\x1b[1;36m".to_string()
                    };
                    current_line.push_str(&heading_style);
                    current_line.push_str(prefix);
                    active_heading_style = Some(heading_style);
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Heading(_)) => {
                    active_heading_style = None;
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[0m");
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Paragraph) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Paragraph) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::BlockQuote(_)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    blockquote_depth += 1;
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::BlockQuote(_)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    blockquote_depth = blockquote_depth.saturating_sub(1);
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::CodeBlock(kind)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    in_code_block = true;
                    code_block_lang = match kind {
                        CodeBlockKind::Fenced(lang) => sanitize_markdown_text(&lang, false),
                        CodeBlockKind::Indented => String::new(),
                    };
                    code_block_buf.clear();
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::CodeBlock) => {
                    in_code_block = false;
                    let clean_code = sanitize_markdown_text(&code_block_buf, true);
                    code_block_buf.clear();

                    let raw_lines: Vec<&str> = if clean_code.is_empty() {
                        vec![""]
                    } else {
                        clean_code.lines().collect()
                    };

                    let effective_w = width.saturating_sub(blockquote_depth * 2).max(1);
                    let mut block_lines = Vec::new();

                    if effective_w < 5 {
                        for line in raw_lines {
                            for wrapped in wrap_text_with_ansi(line, effective_w) {
                                block_lines.push(format!("{code_color}{wrapped}\x1b[0m"));
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        let middle_w = effective_w - 2;
                        let inner_w = effective_w - 4;
                        let lang_tag = if code_block_lang.trim().is_empty() {
                            String::new()
                        } else {
                            crate::ansi::truncate_to_width(
                                &format!(" {} ", code_block_lang.trim()),
                                middle_w.saturating_sub(1),
                                "…",
                                false,
                            )
                        };
                        let lang_w = visible_width(&lang_tag);
                        let right_dashes = middle_w.saturating_sub(1 + lang_w);
                        let top = format!(
                            "{border_color}┌─{lang_tag}{}┐\x1b[0m",
                            "─".repeat(right_dashes)
                        );
                        block_lines.push(top);

                        for line in raw_lines {
                            let wrapped = wrap_text_with_ansi(line, inner_w);
                            for wline in wrapped {
                                let fitted =
                                    crate::ansi::truncate_to_width(&wline, inner_w, "", true);
                                block_lines.push(format!(
                                    "{border_color}│\x1b[0m {code_color}{fitted}\x1b[0m {border_color}│\x1b[0m"
                                ));
                            }
                        }

                        let bottom =
                            format!("{border_color}└{}┘\x1b[0m", "─".repeat(middle_w));
                        block_lines.push(bottom);
                    }

                    push_blockquote_lines(
                        &mut output_lines,
                        block_lines,
                        blockquote_depth,
                        quote_color,
                    );
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Table(_)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    in_table = true;
                    table_has_header = false;
                    table_rows.clear();
                    table_current_row.clear();
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Table) => {
                    in_table = false;
                    let effective_w = width.saturating_sub(blockquote_depth * 2).max(1);
                    let table_lines = format_markdown_table(
                        &table_rows,
                        table_has_header,
                        effective_w,
                        border_color,
                    );
                    push_blockquote_lines(
                        &mut output_lines,
                        table_lines,
                        blockquote_depth,
                        quote_color,
                    );
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                    table_rows.clear();
                    table_current_row.clear();
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::TableHead) => {
                    table_current_row.clear();
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::TableHead) => {
                    table_has_header = true;
                    table_rows.push(std::mem::take(&mut table_current_row));
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::TableRow) => {
                    table_current_row.clear();
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::TableRow) => {
                    table_rows.push(std::mem::take(&mut table_current_row));
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::TableCell) => {
                    current_line.clear();
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::TableCell) => {
                    table_current_row.push(std::mem::take(&mut current_line).trim().to_string());
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::List(first_num)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    list_stack.push(first_num);
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::List(_)) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    list_stack.pop();
                    if list_stack.is_empty() {
                        output_lines.push(String::new());
                    }
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Item) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    let depth = list_stack.len().saturating_sub(1);
                    let indent = "  ".repeat(depth);
                    if let Some(Some(ref mut n)) = list_stack.last_mut() {
                        current_line.push_str(&format!("{indent}{n}. "));
                        *n += 1;
                    } else {
                        current_line.push_str(&format!("{indent}- "));
                    }
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Item) => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                }
                Event::TaskListMarker(checked) => {
                    if checked {
                        current_line.push_str("\x1b[32m[x]\x1b[0m ");
                    } else {
                        current_line.push_str("\x1b[2m[ ]\x1b[0m ");
                    }
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Emphasis) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[3m");
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Emphasis) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[23m");
                    if let Some(ref h_style) = active_heading_style {
                        current_line.push_str(h_style);
                    }
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Strong) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[1m");
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Strong) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[22m");
                    if let Some(ref h_style) = active_heading_style {
                        current_line.push_str(h_style);
                    }
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Strikethrough) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[9m");
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Strikethrough) => {
                    current_line.push_str("\x1b[29m");
                    if let Some(ref h_style) = active_heading_style {
                        current_line.push_str(h_style);
                    }
                }
                Event::Start(Tag::Link { dest_url, .. }) => {
                    let link_style = if !self.theme.md_link.is_empty() {
                        &self.theme.md_link
                    } else {
                        "\x1b[4;34m"
                    };
                    if let Some(safe_url) = sanitize_osc8_url(&dest_url) {
                        current_line.push_str(&format!("\x1b]8;;{safe_url}\x07{link_style}"));
                        link_osc8_stack.push(true);
                    } else {
                        current_line.push_str(link_style);
                        link_osc8_stack.push(false);
                    }
                }
                Event::End(TagEnd::Link) => {
                    let opened = link_osc8_stack.pop().unwrap_or(false);
                    let restore = active_heading_style.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
                    if opened {
                        current_line.push_str(&format!("\x1b]8;;\x07\x1b[0m{restore}"));
                    } else {
                        current_line.push_str(&format!("\x1b[0m{restore}"));
                    }
                }
                Event::Code(text) => {
                    if in_code_block {
                        code_block_buf.push_str(&text);
                    } else {
                        let clean = sanitize_markdown_text(&text, false);
                        if let Some(ref h_style) = active_heading_style {
                            current_line.push_str(&format!("{code_color}{clean}\x1b[0m{h_style}"));
                        } else {
                            current_line.push_str(&format!("{code_color}{clean}\x1b[0m"));
                        }
                    }
                }
                Event::Text(text) => {
                    if in_code_block {
                        code_block_buf.push_str(&text);
                    } else {
                        current_line.push_str(&sanitize_markdown_text(&text, false));
                    }
                }
                Event::Rule => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                    let rule_w = width.min(40);
                    output_lines.push(format!("{border_color}{}\x1b[0m", "─".repeat(rule_w)));
                    output_lines.push(String::new());
                }
                Event::SoftBreak if !in_code_block => {
                    current_line.push(' ');
                }
                Event::HardBreak if !in_code_block && !in_table => {
                    flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        flush_current_line(&mut current_line, &mut output_lines, blockquote_depth);

        // Strip trailing empty lines
        while output_lines.last().map(|l| l.is_empty()).unwrap_or(false) {
            output_lines.pop();
        }

        // Clean wrap to target terminal width
        let mut final_lines = Vec::new();
        for line in output_lines {
            if line.is_empty() {
                final_lines.push(String::new());
            } else {
                for wrapped in wrap_text_with_ansi(&line, width) {
                    final_lines.push(wrapped);
                }
            }
        }

        final_lines
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::ansi::strip_ansi;

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_renders_tables_and_tasklists() {
        let src = "\
| Name | Status |
| --- | --- |
| Auth | OK |

- [x] Done task
- [ ] Pending task";
        let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(50);
        let plain = lines
            .iter()
            .map(|l| strip_ansi(l))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n");

        assert!(plain.contains("Name"));
        assert!(plain.contains("Auth"));
        assert!(plain.contains("──"));
        assert!(plain.contains("[x] Done task"));
        assert!(plain.contains("[ ] Pending task"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_strips_terminal_control_sequences() {
        let src = "Hello \x1b[31mRED\x1b[0m and \x1b]2;pwned\x07world\n\n```rust\nlet s = \"\x1b[2Jclear\";\n```";
        let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(60);
        let joined = lines.join("\n");
        assert!(!joined.contains("\x1b[31m"));
        assert!(!joined.contains("\x1b]2;pwned"));
        assert!(!joined.contains("\x1b[2J"));
        let plain: String = lines.iter().map(|l| strip_ansi(l)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        assert!(plain.contains("Hello RED and world"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_code_block_fits_narrow_width() {
        let src = "```rust\nlet very_long_identifier = \"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz\";\n```";
        for w in [10usize, 24, 40] {
            let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(w);
            for line in &lines {
                assert!(
                    visible_width(line) <= w,
                    "Line width {} exceeded box width {w}: {line:?}",
                    visible_width(line)
                );
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_heading_preserves_styling_after_inline_code() {
        let src = "### Why `sourceInfo` should not be optional";
        let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(80);
        let joined = lines.join("\n");
        assert!(joined.contains("\x1b[33m")); // yellow inline code
        let code_marker = "sourceInfo\x1b[0m";
        let code_end = joined.find(code_marker).unwrap() + code_marker.len();
        let after_idx = joined.find("should not be optional").unwrap();
        let between = &joined[code_end..after_idx];
        assert!(
            between.contains("\x1b[1;36m") || between.contains("\x1b[36m"),
            "Heading style must be re-applied immediately after inline code: {between:?}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_table_narrow_width_does_not_overflow() {
        let src = "| ColumnOne | ColumnTwo |\n| --- | --- |\n| very_long_value_one | very_long_value_two |";
        // Narrow width smaller than overhead
        let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(6);
        for line in &lines {
            // Should not produce an unconstrained 100-character line
            assert!(
                crate::ansi::visible_width(line) <= 12,
                "Table line exceeded expected bounds: {:?}",
                line
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_markdown_softbreak_preserves_space() {
        let src = "Hello\nworld";
        let lines = MarkdownView::new(src).render(40);
        let plain = lines
            .iter()
            .map(|l| strip_ansi(l))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n");
        assert!(plain.contains("Hello world"), "Paragraph softbreak must have space between lines: {plain}");
    }
}

FILE: crates/mehh-tui/src/components/settings_list.rs
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
//! Two-column settings list with description pane and value cycling,
//! ported from TypeScript `packages/tui/src/components/settings-list.ts`.

use crate::ansi::{truncate_to_width, visible_width, wrap_text_with_ansi};
use crate::fuzzy::fuzzy_filter_indexed;
use crate::tui::Component;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SettingItem {
    pub id: String,
    pub label: String,
    pub description: Option<String>,
    pub current_value: String,
    pub values: Vec<String>,
}

impl SettingItem {
    pub fn new(
        id: impl Into<String>,
        label: impl Into<String>,
        current_value: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: id.into(),
            label: label.into(),
            description: None,
            current_value: current_value.into(),
            values: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn with_description(mut self, desc: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.description = Some(desc.into());
        self
    }

    pub fn with_values(mut self, values: Vec<String>) -> Self {
        self.values = values;
        self
    }
}

/// Two-column interactive settings list.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SettingsList {
    pub items: Vec<SettingItem>,
    pub selected: usize,
    pub max_visible: usize,
    pub filter: String,
}

impl SettingsList {
    pub fn new(items: Vec<SettingItem>, max_visible: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            items,
            selected: 0,
            max_visible: max_visible.max(1),
            filter: String::new(),
        }
    }

    pub fn set_items(&mut self, items: Vec<SettingItem>) {
        self.items = items;
        self.selected = self.selected.min(self.items.len().saturating_sub(1));
    }

    pub fn set_filter(&mut self, filter: &str) {
        self.filter = filter.to_string();
        self.selected = 0;
    }

    pub fn update_value(&mut self, id: &str, new_value: &str) {
        if let Some(item) = self.items.iter_mut().find(|it| it.id == id) {
            item.current_value = new_value.to_string();
        }
    }

    pub fn filtered_indices(&self) -> Vec<usize> {
        if self.filter.trim().is_empty() {
            return (0..self.items.len()).collect();
        }
        fuzzy_filter_indexed(&self.items, &self.filter, |item| {
            if let Some(desc) = &item.description {
                format!("{} {} {} {}", item.label, item.id, item.current_value, desc)
            } else {
                format!("{} {} {}", item.label, item.id, item.current_value)
            }
        })
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(idx, _)| idx)
        .collect()
    }

    pub fn move_up(&mut self) {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if !filtered.is_empty() {
            let cur = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
            if cur == 0 {
                self.selected = filtered.len() - 1;
            } else {
                self.selected = cur - 1;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn move_down(&mut self) {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if !filtered.is_empty() {
            let cur = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
            self.selected = (cur + 1) % filtered.len();
        }
    }

    pub fn page_up(&mut self) {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let cur = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
        self.selected = cur.saturating_sub(self.max_visible);
    }

    pub fn page_down(&mut self) {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let cur = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
        self.selected = (cur + self.max_visible).min(filtered.len() - 1);
    }

    pub fn selected_item(&self) -> Option<&SettingItem> {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let sel = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
        Some(&self.items[filtered[sel]])
    }

    pub fn selected_item_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut SettingItem> {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let sel = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
        let idx = filtered[sel];
        self.items.get_mut(idx)
    }

    /// Cycle the currently selected item's value to the next in `values`.
    /// Returns `Some((id, new_value))` if a cycle occurred.
    pub fn cycle_current(&mut self) -> Option<(String, String)> {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let sel = self.selected.min(filtered.len() - 1);
        let idx = filtered[sel];
        let item = self.items.get_mut(idx)?;

        if item.values.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let curr_idx = item
            .values
            .iter()
            .position(|v| v == &item.current_value)
            .unwrap_or(0);
        let next_idx = (curr_idx + 1) % item.values.len();
        let new_val = item.values[next_idx].clone();
        item.current_value = new_val.clone();
        Some((item.id.clone(), new_val))
    }
}

impl Component for SettingsList {
    fn render(&self, width: usize) -> Vec<String> {
        let filtered = self.filtered_indices();
        if filtered.is_empty() {
            return vec![truncate_to_width("  No matching settings", width, "...", false)];
        }

        let total = filtered.len();
        let selected = self.selected.min(total.saturating_sub(1));
        let max_vis = self.max_visible.min(total);
        let start = (selected as isize - (max_vis as isize / 2))
            .clamp(0, (total as isize - max_vis as isize).max(0)) as usize;
        let end = (start + max_vis).min(total);

        let max_label_width = self
            .items
            .iter()
            .map(|it| visible_width(&it.label))
            .max()
            .unwrap_or(12)
            .clamp(10, 36);

        let mut lines = Vec::new();
        for (vis_idx, &item_idx) in filtered[start..end].iter().enumerate() {
            let actual_idx = start + vis_idx;
            let item = &self.items[item_idx];
            let is_selected = actual_idx == selected;

            let prefix = if is_selected {
                "\x1b[36m> \x1b[0m"
            } else {
                "  "
            };

            let label_len = visible_width(&item.label);
            let pad = if max_label_width > label_len {
                " ".repeat(max_label_width - label_len)
            } else {
                String::new()
            };

            let label_styled = if is_selected {
                format!("\x1b[1m{}{pad}\x1b[0m", item.label)
            } else {
                format!("{}{pad}", item.label)
            };

            let value_styled = if is_selected {
                format!("\x1b[36m{}\x1b[0m", item.current_value)
            } else {
                format!("\x1b[2m{}\x1b[0m", item.current_value)
            };

            let row = format!("{prefix}{label_styled}  {value_styled}");
            lines.push(truncate_to_width(&row, width, "...", false));
        }

        if total > max_vis {
            lines.push(truncate_to_width(
                &format!("  \x1b[2m({}/{})\x1b[0m", selected + 1, total),
                width,
                "...",
                false,
            ));
        }

        let selected_item = self.selected_item();
        if let Some(desc) = selected_item.and_then(|it| it.description.as_deref()) {
            lines.push(String::new());
            let wrapped = wrap_text_with_ansi(desc, width.saturating_sub(4).max(10));
            for w in wrapped {
                lines.push(truncate_to_width(&format!("  \x1b[2m{w}\x1b[0m"), width, "...", false));
            }
        }

        lines.push(String::new());
        lines.push(truncate_to_width(
            "  \x1b[2mEnter/Space to change · Esc to close\x1b[0m",
            width,
            "...",
            false,
        ));

        lines
    }

    fn handle_key(&mut self, key: &str) -> bool {
        match key {
            "up" => {
                self.move_up();
                true
            }
            "down" => {
                self.move_down();
                true
            }
            "pageup" => {
                self.page_up();
                true
            }
            "pagedown" => {
                self.page_down();
                true
            }
            "enter" | " " | "return" => self.cycle_current().is_some(),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_settings_list_render_and_cycle() {
        let items = vec![
            SettingItem::new("theme", "Color theme", "dark")
                .with_values(vec!["dark".into(), "light".into()])
                .with_description("Active color theme for the TUI"),
            SettingItem::new("mode", "Queue mode", "none")
                .with_values(vec!["none".into(), "all".into(), "one_at_a_time".into()]),
        ];
        let mut list = SettingsList::new(items, 5);

        let lines = list.render(60);
        assert!(lines[0].contains("> "));
        assert!(lines[0].contains("Color theme"));
        assert!(lines[0].contains("dark"));
        assert!(lines.iter().any(|l| l.contains("Active color theme for the TUI")));

        // Cycle theme
        let cycled = list.cycle_current();
        assert_eq!(cycled, Some(("theme".to_string(), "light".to_string())));
        assert_eq!(list.items[0].current_value, "light");

        // Move down and cycle queue mode
        list.move_down();
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 1);
        let cycled2 = list.cycle_current();
        assert_eq!(cycled2, Some(("mode".to_string(), "all".to_string())));
        assert_eq!(list.items[1].current_value, "all");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_settings_list_fuzzy_filter() {
        let items = vec![
            SettingItem::new("theme", "Color theme", "dark"),
            SettingItem::new("steering", "Steering mode", "none"),
        ];
        let mut list = SettingsList::new(items, 5);
        list.set_filter("steer");
        assert_eq!(list.filtered_indices(), vec![1]);
        assert_eq!(list.selected_item().unwrap().id, "steering");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_settings_list_out_of_bounds_selected_render_does_not_panic() {
        let items = vec![
            SettingItem::new("theme", "Color theme", "dark")
                .with_values(vec!["dark".into(), "light".into()]),
        ];
        let mut list = SettingsList::new(items, 5);
        list.selected = 100;
        let lines = list.render(60);
        assert!(!lines.is_empty());
        assert!(lines[0].contains("> "));
        assert!(lines[0].contains("Color theme"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected_item().map(|it| it.id.as_str()), Some("theme"));
        let cycled = list.cycle_current();
        assert_eq!(cycled, Some(("theme".to_string(), "light".to_string())));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_settings_list_viewport_centering() {
        let items: Vec<SettingItem> = (0..10)
            .map(|i| SettingItem::new(format!("id_{i}"), format!("Setting {i}"), "val"))
            .collect();
        let mut list = SettingsList::new(items, 5);
        list.selected = 3;
        let lines = list.render(60);
        // Centered window for selected=3 with max_visible=5 should start at Setting 1
        assert!(lines[0].contains("Setting 1"));
        assert!(lines[2].contains("Setting 3"));
        assert!(lines[2].contains("> "));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_settings_list_page_up_page_down() {
        let items: Vec<SettingItem> = (0..12)
            .map(|i| SettingItem::new(format!("id_{i}"), format!("Setting {i}"), "val"))
            .collect();
        let mut list = SettingsList::new(items, 5);
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 0);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pagedown"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 5);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pagedown"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 10);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pagedown"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 11);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pageup"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 6);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pageup"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 1);

        assert!(list.handle_key("pageup"));
        assert_eq!(list.selected, 0);
    }
}

//! Provides conversational transcript, live markdown streaming, slash commands,

/// One child row inside a nested subagent group, keyed by `child_id`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct SubagentChildRow {
    pub child_id: String,
    pub label: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub detail: String,
}

/// Transcript display entry in the interactive session.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum TranscriptItem {
    User(String),
    Assistant { thinking: String, text: String },
    ToolCall {
        id: String,
        name: String,
        status: String,
        is_error: bool,
        args_summary: String,

        is_error: bool,
        children: Vec<SubagentChildRow>,
    },
    System(String),
}

/// The kind of picker overlay currently open above the transcript.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SelectorKind {
    Model,
    Tree,
    Resume,
    Settings,
    Login,
    ToolApproval,
}

/// A picker overlay: the list of choices plus the context needed to apply one.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Selector {
    pub kind: SelectorKind,
    pub list: SelectList,
    pub settings_list: Option<SettingsList>,
}

type MarkdownCache = HashMap<(usize, usize), (String, String, Vec<String>)>;

/// Context for an active tool approval request awaiting user decision.
pub struct PendingApproval {
    pub tool_name: String,
    pub args: serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>,
    pub reason: String,
    pub preview: Option<String>,
    pub reply_tx: tokio::sync::oneshot::Sender<bool>,
}

/// Interactive broker dispatching tool approval requests to the TUI event loop.
pub struct InteractiveApprovalBroker {
    tx: tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedSender<AgentSignal>,
    approval_lock: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<()>>,
}

impl InteractiveApprovalBroker {
    pub(crate) fn new(tx: tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedSender<AgentSignal>) -> Self {
        Self {
            tx,
            approval_lock: Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(())),
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl crate::tool_inspection::ApprovalHandler for InteractiveApprovalBroker {
    async fn request_approval(
        &self,
        ctx: &crate::tool_inspection::ToolInspectionContext,
        reason: &str,
        preview: Option<&str>,
    ) -> bool {
        let _guard = self.approval_lock.lock().await;
        let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel();
        let pending = PendingApproval {
            tool_name: ctx.tool_name.clone(),
            args: ctx.args.clone(),
            reason: reason.to_string(),
            preview: preview.map(str::to_string),
            reply_tx,
        };
        if self.tx.send(AgentSignal::ToolApprovalRequest(pending)).is_err() {
            return false;
        }
        reply_rx.await.unwrap_or(false)
    }
}

/// State managed by the interactive mode session.
pub struct InteractiveState {
    pub items: Vec<TranscriptItem>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub is_busy: bool,
    pub loader: Loader,
    pub total_input_tokens: u64,
    pub total_output_tokens: u64,
    pub total_cost: f64,
    pub selector: Option<Selector>,
    pub pending_approval: Option<PendingApproval>,
    pub mcp_summaries: Vec<McpConnectionSummary>,
    pub theme: Theme,
    pub keybindings: Keybindings,
    pub scroll_offset: usize,
    pub last_max_scroll: AtomicUsize,
    pub last_prompt_text: Option<String>,
    pub overflow_retried: bool,
    pub turn_start_idx: Option<usize>,
    pub last_turn_error: Option<String>,
    /// File that settings changes are written to. `None` uses the global settings file.
    pub settings_file: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// File that credentials are written to. `None` uses the default auth file.
    pub auth_file: Option<PathBuf>,
    markdown_cache: Mutex<MarkdownCache>,
}

impl InteractiveState {
    pub fn new(mcp_summaries: Vec<McpConnectionSummary>, settings: &Settings) -> Self {
        Self {
            items: Vec::new(),
            status: None,
            is_busy: false,
            loader: Loader::new("Thinking..."),
            total_input_tokens: 0,
            total_output_tokens: 0,
            total_cost: 0.0,
            selector: None,
            pending_approval: None,
            mcp_summaries,
            theme: Theme::load(settings.theme.as_deref()),
            keybindings: Keybindings::default_bindings(),
            scroll_offset: 0,
            last_max_scroll: AtomicUsize::new(0),
            last_prompt_text: None,
            overflow_retried: false,
            turn_start_idx: None,
            last_turn_error: None,
            settings_file: None,
            auth_file: None,
            markdown_cache: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }
}

/// Result of evaluating a slash command.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SlashCommandOutcome {
    NotACommand,
    Handled,
    Compact(Option<String>),
    Quit,
}

/// Routing of editor input submitted while an agent turn is already running.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BusyInput {
    /// Text queued for steering into the running turn.
    Steer(String),
    /// Text queued to run once the current turn finishes.
    FollowUp(String),
}

/// Routes editor input submitted while an agent turn is running.
///
/// `/follow-up <text>` becomes a follow-up message; anything else is queued as a
/// steering message injected into the running turn.
pub fn route_busy_input(input: &str) -> BusyInput {
    let trimmed = input.trim();
    if let Some(rest) = trimmed.strip_prefix("/follow-up") {
        let text = rest.trim();
        // The command name must end at a boundary, so `/follow-upXYZ` is not a match.
        if !text.is_empty() && rest.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) {
            return BusyInput::FollowUp(text.to_string());
        }
    }
    BusyInput::Steer(trimmed.to_string())
}

/// Copies a single interactive toggle from one settings set into another.
///
/// The remaining keys (model, provider, limits) belong to the user's machine or
/// project and are never overwritten by an interactive toggle.
pub fn copy_toggle(target: &mut Settings, source: &Settings, key: &str) {
    match key {
        "theme" => target.theme = source.theme.clone(),
        "steering_mode" => target.steering_mode = source.steering_mode,
        "follow_up_mode" => target.follow_up_mode = source.follow_up_mode,
        "default_thinking_level" => target.default_thinking_level = source.default_thinking_level,
        "hide_thinking_block" => target.hide_thinking_block = source.hide_thinking_block,
        "auto_compact" => target.auto_compact = source.auto_compact,
        _ => {}
    }
}

/// Persists a single interactive setting.
///
/// The target file is loaded first, so a malformed file is reported instead of
/// overwritten, and only the changed key is written, so project-local values of
/// the other keys are not promoted into the global configuration.
pub fn persist_settings(
    settings: &Settings,
    key: &str,
    path: Option<&Path>,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let file_path = match path {
        Some(p) => p.to_path_buf(),
        None => SettingsManager::global_path()?,
    };
    let mut target = SettingsManager::load_file(&file_path)?;
    copy_toggle(&mut target, settings, key);
    SettingsManager::save_file(&file_path, &target)
}

/// Returns true when a theme name can be resolved to a theme.
///
/// Built-in names (`dark`, `light`) always resolve; other names resolve when they
/// point at a readable theme file.
pub fn theme_source_exists(name: &str) -> bool {
    let trimmed = name.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return false;
    }
    if trimmed.eq_ignore_ascii_case("dark") || trimmed.eq_ignore_ascii_case("light") {
        return true;
    }
    if Path::new(trimmed).exists() {
        return true;
    }
    let home = match std::env::var_os("USERPROFILE").or_else(|| std::env::var_os("HOME")) {
        Some(h) => PathBuf::from(h),
        None => return false,
    };
    let filename = if trimmed.ends_with(".json") {
        trimmed.to_string()
    } else {
        format!("{trimmed}.json")
    };
    home.join(".mehh")
        .join("agent")
        .join("themes")
        .join(filename)
        .exists()
}

/// Logs into a provider and stores the resulting credential.
///
/// An explicit API key is stored verbatim; otherwise the provider's OAuth flow is
/// used (PKCE for Anthropic and ChatGPT, device code for GitHub Copilot).
pub async fn perform_login(
    provider: &str,
    api_key: Option<&str>,
    auth_file: Option<&Path>,
) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
    if let Some(key) = api_key.map(str::trim).filter(|k| !k.is_empty()) {
        let cred = StoredCredential::ApiKey {
            key: Some(key.to_string()),
            env: None,
        };
        crate::auth::save_credential(provider, &cred, auth_file)?;
        return Ok(format!("Saved API key for {provider}"));
    }

    let kind = crate::auth::oauth_kind_for_provider(provider).ok_or_else(|| {
        anyhow::anyhow!(
            "Provider '{provider}' does not support OAuth. Provide an API key instead."
        )
    })?;

    let client = mehh_ai::default_http_client();
    let cred = match kind {
        OAuthProviderKind::Anthropic | OAuthProviderKind::OpenAiChatGpt => {
            login_via_pkce(&client, kind).await?
        }
        OAuthProviderKind::GitHubCopilot => login_via_device_flow(&client).await?,
    };

    crate::auth::save_credential(provider, &cred, auth_file)?;
    Ok(format!("Logged in to {provider}"))
}

/// Removes the stored credential for a provider. Returns true when one was removed.
pub fn perform_logout(provider: &str, auth_file: Option<&Path>) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
    crate::auth::remove_credential(provider, auth_file)
}

/// Refreshes the session API key after a login so the running agent uses the new
/// credential without a restart.
///
/// Only the credential of the session's own provider can replace the session key:
/// a key stored for another provider would be sent to the wrong endpoint.
pub async fn refresh_session_api_key(
    session: &mut AgentSession,
    provider: &str,
    auth_file: Option<&Path>,
) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    if !provider.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&session.model.provider) {
        return Ok(());
    }

    // The credential that was just stored wins over the environment variable.
    let entries = crate::auth::load_auth_entries(auth_file)?;
    let key = entries
        .get(provider)
        .or_else(|| {
            entries
                .iter()
                .find(|(k, _)| k.eq_ignore_ascii_case(provider))
                .map(|(_, v)| v)
        })
        .and_then(|cred| match cred {
            StoredCredential::ApiKey { key, .. } => key.clone(),
            StoredCredential::OAuth { access, .. } => Some(access.clone()),
        });

    if let Some(key) = key {
        session.set_api_key(Some(key));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Runs the OAuth PKCE flow against a loopback callback listener.
async fn login_via_pkce(
    client: &reqwest::Client,
    kind: OAuthProviderKind,
) -> anyhow::Result<StoredCredential> {
    let listener = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0")
        .context("Failed to bind loopback listener for OAuth callback")?;
    let port = listener
        .local_addr()
        .context("Failed to read loopback listener address")?
        .port();
    drop(listener);

    let redirect_uri = format!("http://127.0.0.1:{port}/callback");
    let pkce = oauth::generate_pkce();
    let state = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let url = oauth::build_authorize_url(kind, &pkce.challenge, &state, &redirect_uri)?;

    println!("Open the following URL to authenticate:\n{url}");

    let code = oauth::wait_for_loopback_callback(port, &state, Duration::from_secs(300)).await?;
    oauth::exchange_code_for_tokens(client, kind, &code, &pkce.verifier, &redirect_uri).await
}

/// Runs the GitHub Copilot device code flow until the user approves or the code expires.
async fn login_via_device_flow(client: &reqwest::Client) -> anyhow::Result<StoredCredential> {
    let challenge = oauth::start_copilot_device_flow(client).await?;
    println!(
        "Enter code {} at {}",
        challenge.user_code, challenge.verification_uri
    );

    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(challenge.expires_in_secs);
    let interval = Duration::from_secs(challenge.interval_secs.max(1));

    let mut poll_interval = interval;
    while std::time::Instant::now() < deadline {
        tokio::time::sleep(poll_interval).await;
        match oauth::poll_copilot_device_token(client, &challenge.device_code).await? {
            oauth::CopilotDevicePoll::Complete(cred) => return Ok(cred),
            oauth::CopilotDevicePoll::SlowDown => {
                poll_interval += Duration::from_secs(5);
            }
            oauth::CopilotDevicePoll::Pending => {}
        }
    }

    anyhow::bail!("Device code login timed out before it was approved")
}

/// Converts a persisted message into its transcript representation.
fn message_to_transcript_item(message: &Message) -> TranscriptItem {
    match message {
        Message::User(u) => TranscriptItem::User(u.content.text()),
        Message::Assistant(a) => TranscriptItem::Assistant {
            thinking: a
                .content
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|b| match b {
                    AssistantContentBlock::Thinking(t) => Some(t.thinking.as_str()),
                    _ => None,
                })
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n"),
            text: a.text_content(),
        },
        Message::ToolResult(tr) => TranscriptItem::ToolCall {
            id: tr.tool_call_id.clone(),
            name: tr.tool_name.clone(),
            status: if tr.is_error {
                "failed".to_string()
            } else {
                "completed".to_string()
            },
            is_error: tr.is_error,
            args_summary: String::new(),
            output_preview: Vec::new(),
            diff: tr.details.as_ref().and_then(|d| d.get("diff")).and_then(|v| v.as_str()).map(str::to_string),
        },
        Message::System(s) => TranscriptItem::System(s.content.clone()),
    }
}

/// Rebuilds the transcript entries from the session's active branch.
pub fn rebuild_transcript_items(session: &AgentSession) -> Vec<TranscriptItem> {
    session
        .session()
        .active_path_entries()
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|entry| match &entry.kind {
            SessionEntryKind::Message { message } => Some(message_to_transcript_item(message)),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Human readable label for a queue mode.
fn queue_mode_label(mode: QueueMode) -> &'static str {
    match mode {
        QueueMode::OneAtATime => "one-at-a-time",
        QueueMode::All => "all",
    }
}

/// Human readable label for a thinking level.
pub fn thinking_level_label(level: ThinkingLevel) -> &'static str {
    match level {
        ThinkingLevel::Off => "off",
        ThinkingLevel::Minimal => "minimal",
        ThinkingLevel::Low => "low",
        ThinkingLevel::Medium => "medium",
        ThinkingLevel::High => "high",
        ThinkingLevel::Xhigh => "xhigh",
        ThinkingLevel::Max => "max",
    }
}

/// Returns the next thinking level in the cycle.
fn cycle_thinking_level(current: Option<ThinkingLevel>) -> ThinkingLevel {
    match current {
        None | Some(ThinkingLevel::Off) => ThinkingLevel::Low,
        Some(ThinkingLevel::Minimal) | Some(ThinkingLevel::Low) => ThinkingLevel::Medium,
        Some(ThinkingLevel::Medium) => ThinkingLevel::High,
        Some(ThinkingLevel::High) | Some(ThinkingLevel::Xhigh) | Some(ThinkingLevel::Max) => {
            ThinkingLevel::Off
        }
    }
}

/// Builds the picker entries for the settings screen.
fn settings_selector_items(settings: &Settings) -> Vec<SelectItem> {
    vec![
        SelectItem::new(
            "theme",
            format!(
                "theme: {}",
                settings.theme.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "dark".to_string())
            ),
        ),
        SelectItem::new(
            "steering_mode",
            format!(
                "steering mode: {}",
                queue_mode_label(settings.steering_mode.unwrap_or_default())
            ),
        ),
        SelectItem::new(
            "follow_up_mode",
            format!(
                "follow-up mode: {}",
                queue_mode_label(settings.follow_up_mode.unwrap_or_default())
            ),
        ),
        SelectItem::new(
            "default_thinking_level",
            format!(
                "thinking level: {}",
                settings
                    .default_thinking_level
                    .map(thinking_level_label)
                    .unwrap_or("off")
            ),
        ),
        SelectItem::new(
            "hide_thinking_block",
            format!(
                "hide thinking block: {}",
                settings.hide_thinking_block.unwrap_or(false)
            ),
        ),
        SelectItem::new(
            "auto_compact",
            format!("auto compact: {}", settings.auto_compact.unwrap_or(true)),
        ),
    ]
}

/// Builds the two-column settings list component for the settings screen.
fn build_settings_list(settings: &Settings) -> SettingsList {
    let items = vec![
        SettingItem::new(
            "theme",
            "theme",
            settings.theme.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "dark".to_string()),
        )
        .with_values(vec!["dark".to_string(), "light".to_string()])
        .with_description("Active color theme for the TUI"),
        SettingItem::new(
            "steering_mode",
            "steering mode",
            queue_mode_label(settings.steering_mode.unwrap_or_default()),
        )
        .with_values(vec![
            "none".to_string(),
            "all".to_string(),
            "one_at_a_time".to_string(),
        ])
        .with_description("Steering mode for tool execution and prompts"),
        SettingItem::new(
            "follow_up_mode",
            "follow-up mode",
            queue_mode_label(settings.follow_up_mode.unwrap_or_default()),
        )
        .with_values(vec![
            "none".to_string(),
            "all".to_string(),
            "one_at_a_time".to_string(),
        ])
        .with_description("Follow-up queue mode"),
        SettingItem::new(
            "default_thinking_level",
            "default thinking level",
            settings
                .default_thinking_level
                .map(|t| format!("{t:?}").to_lowercase())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "off".to_string()),
        )
        .with_values(vec![
            "off".to_string(),
            "low".to_string(),
            "medium".to_string(),
            "high".to_string(),
        ])
        .with_description("Default thinking budget level"),
        SettingItem::new(
            "hide_thinking_block",
            "hide thinking block",
            if settings.hide_thinking_block.unwrap_or(false) {
                "true"
            } else {
                "false"
            },
        )
        .with_values(vec!["false".to_string(), "true".to_string()])
        .with_description("Collapse or hide thinking blocks in output"),
        SettingItem::new(
            "auto_compact",
            "auto compact",
            if settings.auto_compact.unwrap_or(true) {
                "true"
            } else {
                "false"
            },
        )
        .with_values(vec!["true".to_string(), "false".to_string()])
        .with_description("Automatically compact context when nearing limit"),
    ];
    SettingsList::new(items, 8)
}

/// Cycles a single setting value and applies it to the running session.
fn apply_setting_cycle(
    key: &str,
    session: &mut AgentSession,
    state: &mut InteractiveState,
) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
    match key {
        "theme" => {
            let current = session
                .settings
                .theme
                .clone()
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "dark".to_string());
            let next = if current.eq_ignore_ascii_case("light") {
                "dark".to_string()
            } else {
                "light".to_string()
            };
            state.theme = Theme::load(Some(&next));
            session.settings.theme = Some(next.clone());
            Ok(format!("theme = {next}"))
        }
        "steering_mode" => {
            let current = session.settings.steering_mode.unwrap_or_default();
            let next = match current {
                QueueMode::OneAtATime => QueueMode::All,
                QueueMode::All => QueueMode::OneAtATime,
            };
            session.settings.steering_mode = Some(next);
            Ok(format!("steering_mode = {}", queue_mode_label(next)))
        }
        "follow_up_mode" => {
            let current = session.settings.follow_up_mode.unwrap_or_default();
            let next = match current {
                QueueMode::OneAtATime => QueueMode::All,
                QueueMode::All => QueueMode::OneAtATime,
            };
            session.settings.follow_up_mode = Some(next);
            Ok(format!("follow_up_mode = {}", queue_mode_label(next)))
        }
        "default_thinking_level" => {
            let next = cycle_thinking_level(session.settings.default_thinking_level);
            session.settings.default_thinking_level = Some(next);
            session.set_thinking_level(if next == ThinkingLevel::Off {
                None
            } else {
                Some(next)
            });
            Ok(format!(
                "default_thinking_level = {}",
                thinking_level_label(next)
            ))
        }
        "hide_thinking_block" => {
            let next = !session.settings.hide_thinking_block.unwrap_or(false);
            session.settings.hide_thinking_block = Some(next);
            Ok(format!("hide_thinking_block = {next}"))
        }
        "auto_compact" => {
            let next = !session.settings.auto_compact.unwrap_or(true);
            session.settings.auto_compact = Some(next);
            Ok(format!("auto_compact = {next}"))
        }
        other => anyhow::bail!("Unknown setting: '{other}'"),
    }
}

/// Splits a rendered system prompt into its preamble and its addendum.
fn split_system_prompt(prompt: &str) -> (Option<String>, Option<String>) {
    let mut sections = prompt.split("\n\n");
    let preamble = sections.next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
    let custom = if preamble.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some(preamble)
    };

    let mut addendum = None;
    for section in sections {
        if let Some(inner) = section
            .strip_prefix("<addendum>\n")
            .and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("\n</addendum>"))
        {
            addendum = Some(inner.to_string());
        }
    }

    (custom, addendum)
}

/// Replaces the MCP-derived tools on the session with a freshly synchronized set.
///
/// Preserves multi-paragraph custom/append system prompts when stored on `AgentSession`,
/// falling back to `split_system_prompt` only when no explicit overrides were recorded.
fn sync_mcp_tools(
    session: &mut AgentSession,
    mcp_tools: Vec<Arc<dyn AgentTool>>,
    codemode_enabled: bool,
) {
    // `AgentSession::sync_mcp_tools` preserves the CodemodeTool and deferred
    // catalog handles. Carry rendered prompt overrides into its explicit slots
    // so rebuilding the system prompt does not drop them.
    if session.custom_system_prompt.is_none() && session.append_system_prompt.is_none() {
        let (preamble, addendum) = split_system_prompt(&session.system_prompt);
        session.custom_system_prompt = preamble;
        session.append_system_prompt = addendum;
    }
    session.sync_mcp_tools(mcp_tools, codemode_enabled);
}

/// Switches the session's model via [`ModelRegistry`] and asynchronously refreshes credentials.
async fn apply_interactive_model_switch(
    target: &str,
    session: &mut AgentSession,
    state: &mut InteractiveState,
) {
    let reg = match crate::models_config::ModelRegistry::load_default() {
        Ok(r) => r,
        Err(e) => {
            state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                "Failed to load models registry: {e}"
            )));
            return;
        }
    };
    let Some(m) = reg.resolve(target, None) else {
        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
            "Unknown model: '{target}'"
        )));
        return;
    };

    let resolved_key = if m.provider != "faux" && m.api != "faux" {
        match crate::auth::resolve_api_key_async(&m.provider, None, state.auth_file.as_deref()).await
        {
            Ok(key) => key.or_else(|| reg.provider_api_key(&m.provider).map(str::to_string)),
            Err(e) => {
                state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                    "Failed to resolve credentials for '{}': {e}",
                    m.provider
                )));
                return;
            }
        }
    } else {
        None
    };

    let provider_changed = !session.model.provider.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&m.provider);
    let prov = AgentSession::default_provider_for_model(&m);
    session.set_model(m.clone(), prov);
    if let Some(key) = resolved_key {
        if provider_changed || !session.has_explicit_api_key() {
            session.set_api_key(Some(key));
        }
    }
    state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
        "Switched model to {} ({})",
        m.name, m.provider
    )));
}

/// Formats active keyboard shortcuts from Keybindings for display.
pub fn format_hotkeys(keybindings: &Keybindings) -> String {
    let mut out = String::from("Keyboard Shortcuts:\n");
    let actions = [
        (TuiAction::Submit, "Submit prompt"),
        (TuiAction::Newline, "Insert newline"),
        (TuiAction::Interrupt, "Cancel turn / Interrupt"),
        (TuiAction::ClearOrExit, "Exit interactive session"),
        (TuiAction::TabComplete, "Autocomplete slash commands / files"),
        (TuiAction::ScrollUp, "Scroll up"),
        (TuiAction::ScrollDown, "Scroll down"),
        (TuiAction::HistoryPrev, "Previous history item"),
        (TuiAction::HistoryNext, "Next history item"),
        (TuiAction::Undo, "Undo"),
    ];

    for (action, desc) in actions {
        if let Some(keys) = keybindings.get_bindings(action) {
            if !keys.is_empty() {
                let keys_str = keys.join(", ");
                out.push_str(&format!("  {:<24} {}\n", keys_str, desc));
            }
        }
    }
    out.trim_end().to_string()
}

/// Dispatches interactive slash commands.
pub async fn handle_slash_command(
    input: &str,
    session: &mut AgentSession,
    state: &mut InteractiveState,
    mcp_manager: Option<&mut McpManager>,
    codemode_enabled: bool,
) -> SlashCommandOutcome {
    let trimmed = input.trim();
    if !trimmed.starts_with('/') {
        return SlashCommandOutcome::NotACommand;
    }

    let mut parts = trimmed.split_whitespace();
    let cmd = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
    let arg = parts.next();
    let rest = parts.collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
    let full_arg = trimmed.strip_prefix(cmd).map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty());

    match cmd {
        "/exit" | "/quit" => SlashCommandOutcome::Quit,
        "/clear" => {
            state.items.clear();
            state.scroll_offset = 0;
            state.status = None;
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/help" => {
            let help_text = "\
Commands:
  /help                    Show this help message
  /new                     Start a fresh session
  /compact [instructions]  Compact session history
  /model [model_id]        Switch active model (or open picker)
  /thinking [level]        Set thinking level (off, low, medium, high)
  /theme [name]            Set color theme (dark, light, or theme file name)
  /settings                Browse settings and cycle their values
  /tools                   List all active tools
  /mcp [status|reconnect [name]|auth <name>|resources]
                           Inspect and manage MCP servers
  /tree [entry_id]         Browse session branches and switch between them
  /fork [entry_id]         Fork the session into a new one
  /clone                   Clone the current session
  /resume                  Resume a saved session
  /login [provider] [key]  Log in to a provider (OAuth or API key)
  /logout [provider]       Remove a stored provider credential
  /follow-up <text>        Queue a message for after the current turn
  /clear                   Clear transcript
  /exit or /quit           Exit interactive mode

Keybindings:
  Enter        Submit prompt (while busy: queue steering message)
  Shift+Enter  Insert newline
  Alt+Enter    Queue follow-up message while busy
  Ctrl+C       Interrupt the turn, clear the input, or exit on an empty prompt
  Esc          Interrupt the turn, clear the input, or exit on an empty prompt
  Up / Down    Navigate history or autocomplete";
            state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(help_text.to_string()));
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/mode" => {
            if let Some(mode_str) = full_arg {
                match mode_str.parse::<crate::tool_inspection::PermissionMode>() {
                    Ok(new_mode) => {
                        session.inspection_manager.set_mode(new_mode).await;
                        session.settings.permission_mode = Some(new_mode);
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!("Permission mode set to: {:?}", new_mode)));
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(e));
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let current = session.inspection_manager.mode().await;
                state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                    "Current permission mode: {:?} (options: auto, smart_approve, approve, chat)",
                    current
                )));
            }
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/new" => {
            session.clear_queues();
            match session.session_mut().new_session() {
                Ok(()) => {
                    state.items.clear();
                    state.scroll_offset = 0;
                    state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                        "Started new session: {}",
                        session.session().session_id()
                    )));
                }
                Err(e) => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                    "Failed to start a new session: {e}"
                ))),
            }
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/compact" => {
            let custom_instructions = if let Some(first_arg) = arg {
                let rest_idx = trimmed.find(first_arg).unwrap_or(trimmed.len());
                let rest = trimmed[rest_idx..].trim();
                if rest.is_empty() {
                    None
                } else {
                    Some(rest.to_string())
                }
            } else {
                None
            };
            SlashCommandOutcome::Compact(custom_instructions)
        }
        "/model" => {
            if let Some(target) = arg {
                apply_interactive_model_switch(target, session, state).await;
            } else {
                match crate::models_config::ModelRegistry::load_default() {
                    Ok(reg) => {
                        let items: Vec<SelectItem> = reg
                            .models()
                            .iter()
                            .map(|m| {
                                SelectItem::new(
                                    format!("{}/{}", m.provider, m.id),
                                    format!("{} ({})", m.name, m.provider),
                                )
                                .with_description(m.id.clone())
                            })
                            .collect();
                        state.selector = Some(Selector {
                            kind: SelectorKind::Model,
                            list: SelectList::new(items, 8),
                            settings_list: None,
                        });
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                            "Failed to load models registry: {e}"
                        )));
                    }
                }
            }
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/thinking" => {
            if let Some(level_str) = arg {
                match level_str.to_lowercase().as_str() {
                    "off" | "none" => {
                        session.set_thinking_level(None);
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System("Thinking disabled".to_string()));
                    }
                    "low" => {
                        session.set_thinking_level(Some(ThinkingLevel::Low));
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System("Thinking set to low".to_string()));
                    }
                    "medium" | "med" => {
                        session.set_thinking_level(Some(ThinkingLevel::Medium));
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System("Thinking set to medium".to_string()));
                    }
                    "high" => {
                        session.set_thinking_level(Some(ThinkingLevel::High));
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System("Thinking set to high".to_string()));
                    }
                    other => {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                            "Unknown thinking level: '{other}'. Choose from: off, low, medium, high"
                        )));
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let current = session
                    .thinking_level()
                    .map(thinking_level_label)
                    .unwrap_or("off");
                state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                    "Current thinking level: {current}"
                )));
            }
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/theme" => {
            match arg {
                Some(name) => {
                    if !theme_source_exists(name) {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                            "Unknown theme: '{name}'. Use dark, light, or a theme file name."
                        )));
                    } else {
                        let theme = Theme::load(Some(name));
                        state.theme = theme.clone();
                        session.settings.theme = Some(name.to_string());
                        match persist_settings(
                            &session.settings,
                            "theme",
                            state.settings_file.as_deref(),
                        ) {
                            Ok(()) => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                                "Theme set to '{name}' (active: {})",
                                theme.name
                            ))),
                            Err(e) => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                                "Theme set to '{name}' but saving settings failed: {e}"
                            ))),
                        }
                    }
                }
                None => {
                    state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                        "Current theme: {}",
                        state.theme.name
                    )));
                }
            }
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/settings" => {
            let items = settings_selector_items(&session.settings);
            let sl = build_settings_list(&session.settings);
            state.selector = Some(Selector {
                kind: SelectorKind::Settings,
                list: SelectList::new(items, 8),
                settings_list: Some(sl),
            });
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/tools" => {
            let tool_names: Vec<&str> = session.tools().iter().map(|t| t.name()).collect();
            let summary = format!("Active tools ({}): {}", tool_names.len(), tool_names.join(", "));
            state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(summary));
            SlashCommandOutcome::Handled
        }
        "/mcp" => {
            let sub = arg.map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase());
            match sub.as_deref() {
                None | Some("status") => {
                    if state.mcp_summaries.is_empty() {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                            "No MCP servers configured or connected.".to_string(),
                        ));
                    } else {
                        let mut out = String::from("MCP Servers:\n");
                        for s in &state.mcp_summaries {
                            if let Some(ref err) = s.error {
                                out.push_str(&format!("  - {}: error: {}\n", s.server, err));
                            } else {
                                out.push_str(&format!(
                                    "  - {}: {} tools connected\n",
                                    s.server, s.tool_count
                                ));
                            }
                        }
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(out.trim_end().to_string()));
                    }
                }
                Some("reconnect") => {
                    let name = rest.trim().to_string();
                    match mcp_manager {
                        None => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                            "MCP is not enabled for this session.".to_string(),
                        )),
                        Some(mgr) => {
                            let mut count = 0usize;
                            let mut errors: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
                            if name.is_empty() {
                                for summary in mgr.reconnect_all().await {
                                    count += 1;
                                    if let Some(ref err) = summary.error {
                                        errors.push(format!("{}: {err}", summary.server));
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                match mgr.reconnect_server(&name).await {
                                    Ok(summary) => {
                                        count = 1;
                                        if let Some(ref err) = summary.error {
                                            errors.push(format!("{name}: {err}"));
                                        }
                                    }
                                    Err(e) => errors.push(format!("{name}: {e}")),
                                }
                            }
                            state.mcp_summaries = mgr.summaries().to_vec();
                            let tools = mgr.tools().to_vec();
                            sync_mcp_tools(session, tools, codemode_enabled);
                            let mut msg = format!("Reconnected {count} MCP server(s)");
                            if !errors.is_empty() {
                                msg.push_str(&format!(" — errors: {}", errors.join(", ")));
                            }
                            state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(msg));
                        }
                    }
                }
                Some("auth") => {
                    let name = rest.trim().to_string();
                    if name.is_empty() {
                        state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                            "Usage: /mcp auth <server>".to_string(),
                        ));
                    } else {
                        match mcp_manager {
                            None => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                                "MCP is not enabled for this session.".to_string(),
                            )),
                            Some(mgr) => {
                                let res = mgr
                                    .authenticate_server_with_prompt(&name, |url| {
                                        println!("Open the following URL to authenticate:\n{url}");
                                    })
                                    .await;
                                match res {
                                    Ok(()) => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                                        "Authenticated MCP server '{name}'"
                                    ))),
                                    Err(e) => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                                        "MCP authentication failed for '{name}': {e}"
                                    ))),
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Some("resources") => {
                    match mcp_manager {
                        None => state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                            "MCP is not enabled for this session.".to_string(),
                        )),
                        Some(mgr) => {
                            let registry = mgr.registry();
                            let entries = registry.read().await.clone();
                            if entries.is_empty() {
                                state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(
                                    "No MCP servers are connected.".to_string(),
                                ));
                            } else {
                                let mut out = String::from("MCP Resources:\n");
                                for (server, client) in entries {
                                    match client.list_resources().await {
                                        Ok(resources) if resources.is_empty() => {
                                            out.push_str(&format!("  - {server}: no resources\n"));
                                        }
                                        Ok(resources) => {
                                            for r in resources {
                                                out.push_str(&format!(
                                                    "  - {server}: {} ({})\n",
                                                    r.name, r.uri
                                                ));
                                            }
                                        }
                                        Err(e) => {
                                            out.push_str(&format!("  - {server}: error: {e}\n"));
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(out.trim_end().to_string()));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                Some(other) => {
                    state.items.push(TranscriptItem::System(format!(
                        "Unknown /mcp subcommand: '{other}'. Use status, reconnect, auth or resources."
